<script>
    let currentStep = 1;
    let currentRole = "{{ 'company' if register_type == 'company' else 'agent' }}";
    let selectedDistricts = [];
    let selectedSpecialties = [];

    function switchRegisterRole(role) {
        currentRole = role;
        const btnAgent = document.getElementById('btnRoleAgent');
        const btnCompany = document.getElementById('btnRoleCompany');
        const hiddenFirma = document.getElementById('hiddenFirmaTipi');
        const heading = document.getElementById('pageHeading');
        const inviteBox = document.getElementById('inviteCodeBox');
        const inviteInput = document.getElementById('davet_kodu');
        const lblAd = document.getElementById('lblAd');
        const lblSoyad = document.getElementById('lblSoyad');
        const soyadInput = document.getElementById('soyad');
        const lblCompany = document.getElementById('lblCompany');
        const lblDoc = document.getElementById('lblDocument');
        const avatarLabel = document.getElementById('avatarLabel');
        const districtSec = document.getElementById('districtSection');
        const specialtySec = document.getElementById('specialtySection');

        if (role === 'agent') {
            hiddenFirma.value = 'emlakci';
            btnAgent.className = 'py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition-all duration-200 shadow-sm border border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-white hover:text-emerald-800';
            btnCompany.className = 'py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition-all duration-200 border border-emerald-300 bg-white text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-600 hover:text-white';
            
            heading.innerText = 'Emlakçı Kaydı';
            inviteBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            inviteInput.required = true;
            lblAd.innerText = 'Adınız *';
            lblSoyad.innerText = 'Soyadınız *';
            soyadInput.required = true;
            lblCompany.innerText = 'Şirket / Ofis Adı *';
            lblDoc.innerText = 'Taşınmaz Ticareti Yetki Belgesi';
            avatarLabel.innerText = 'Profil Fotoğrafı Ekle';
            districtSec.classList.remove('hidden');
            specialtySec.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
            hiddenFirma.value = 'sirket';
            btnCompany.className = 'py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition-all duration-200 shadow-sm border border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-white hover:text-emerald-800';
            btnAgent.className = 'py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition-all duration-200 border border-emerald-300 bg-white text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-600 hover:text-white';
            
            heading.innerText = 'Proje Firması / Şirket Kaydı';
            inviteBox.classList.add('hidden');
            inviteInput.required = false;
            inviteInput.value = '';
            lblAd.innerText = 'Yetkili Adı *';
            lblSoyad.innerText = 'Yetkili Soyadı';
            soyadInput.required = false;
            lblCompany.innerText = 'Şirket Ticari Unvanı (Müteahhitlik / Geliştirici) *';
            lblDoc.innerText = 'Vergi Levhası / Faaliyet Belgesi';
            avatarLabel.innerText = 'Şirket Logosu Ekle';
            districtSec.classList.add('hidden');
            specialtySec.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }

    function previewAvatar(event) {
        const file = event.target.files[0];
        if (file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(e) {
                const box = document.getElementById('avatarPreviewBox');
                box.innerHTML = `<img src="${e.target.result}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">`;
            }
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }
    }

    function goToStep2() {
        const ad = document.getElementById('ad').value.trim();
        const eposta = document.getElementById('eposta').value.trim();
        const tel = document.getElementById('telefon').value.trim();
        const pass = document.getElementById('sifre').value;
        const pass2 = document.getElementById('sifre_tekrar').value;
        const kod = document.getElementById('davet_kodu').value.trim();

        if (currentRole === 'agent') {
            const soyad = document.getElementById('soyad').value.trim();
            if (!ad || !soyad || !eposta || !tel || !pass || !kod) {
                alert("Lütfen tüm zorunlu alanları doldurun.");
                return;
            }
        } else {
            if (!ad || !eposta || !tel || !pass) {
                alert("Lütfen zorunlu alanları doldurun.");
                return;
            }
        }

        if (pass !== pass2) {
            document.getElementById('passError').classList.remove('hidden');
            return;
        }
        document.getElementById('passError').classList.add('hidden');

        document.getElementById('sectionStep1').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('sectionStep2').classList.remove('hidden');

        document.getElementById('stepBadge2').className = "w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold shadow-xs";
        document.getElementById('stepText2').className = "text-[10px] font-extrabold text-emerald-800";
        document.getElementById('stepLine').className = "w-8 h-0.5 bg-emerald-600 transition-colors";

        document.getElementById('btnContinue').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('btnSubmit').classList.remove('hidden');
        currentStep = 2;
    }

    function handleBack() {
        if (currentStep === 2) {
            document.getElementById('sectionStep2').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('sectionStep1').classList.remove('hidden');

            document.getElementById('stepBadge2').className = "w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold";
            document.getElementById('stepText2').className = "text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400";
            document.getElementById('stepLine').className = "w-8 h-0.5 bg-slate-200 transition-colors";

            document.getElementById('btnSubmit').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('btnContinue').classList.remove('hidden');
            currentStep = 1;
        } else {
            window.location.href = '/login';
        }
    }

    function toggleDistrict(d, btn) {
        const idx = selectedDistricts.indexOf(d);
        if (idx > -1) {
            selectedDistricts.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = "district-chip px-2.5 py-1 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:border-emerald-300 transition";
            btn.innerText = `+ ${d}`;
        } else {
            selectedDistricts.push(d);
            btn.className = "district-chip px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-50 border-2 border-emerald-600 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-900 transition shadow-xs";
            btn.innerText = `✓ ${d}`;
        }
        document.getElementById('selectedDistrictsInput').value = selectedDistricts.join(',');
        document.getElementById('districtBadge').innerText = `${selectedDistricts.length} seçildi`;
    }

    function toggleSpecialty(sp, btn) {
        const idx = selectedSpecialties.indexOf(sp);
        if (idx > -1) {
            selectedSpecialties.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = "specialty-chip py-2 px-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 text-[11px] font-bold transition";
        } else {
            selectedSpecialties.push(sp);
            btn.className = "specialty-chip py-2 px-1 rounded-xl border-2 border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900 text-[11px] font-extrabold transition shadow-xs";
        }
        document.getElementById('selectedSpecialtiesInput').value = selectedSpecialties.join(',');
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        switchRegisterRole(currentRole);
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    // 1. Proje Tescilleri için Long-Press & Action Sheet Mantığı
    let regTimer = null;
    let isRegLongPress = false;

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-reg-card]').forEach(card => {
        const regId = card.getAttribute('data-reg-id');
        const projectId = card.getAttribute('data-project-id');
        const clientName = card.getAttribute('data-client-name');
        const projectName = card.getAttribute('data-project-name');
        const canCancel = card.getAttribute('data-can-cancel') === 'true';

        // Mobilde Dokunma
        card.addEventListener('touchstart', () => {
            isRegLongPress = false;
            regTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                isRegLongPress = true;
                card.classList.add('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
                if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(50);
                openRegActionSheet(regId, projectId, clientName, projectName, canCancel);
            }, 450);
        }, { passive: true });

        card.addEventListener('touchend', () => {
            clearTimeout(regTimer);
            card.classList.remove('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
            if (!isRegLongPress) {
                // Kısa dokunmada da doğrudan detay açılabilir
                window.location.href = `/projects/detail/${projectId}`;
            }
        });

        card.addEventListener('touchmove', () => {
            clearTimeout(regTimer);
            card.classList.remove('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
        });

        // Masaüstünde Tıklama & Basılı Tutma
        card.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
            if (e.button !== 0) return;
            isRegLongPress = false;
            regTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                isRegLongPress = true;
                openRegActionSheet(regId, projectId, clientName, projectName, canCancel);
            }, 450);
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
            if (e.button !== 0) return;
            clearTimeout(regTimer);
            if (!isRegLongPress) {
                window.location.href = `/projects/detail/${projectId}`;
            }
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => clearTimeout(regTimer));
    });

    function openRegActionSheet(regId, projectId, clientName, projectName, canCancel) {
        document.getElementById('sheetClientName').innerText = clientName;
        document.getElementById('sheetProjectName').innerText = projectName;
        document.getElementById('sheetViewProjectBtn').href = `/projects/detail/${projectId}`;
        
        const cancelForm = document.getElementById('sheetCancelForm');
        cancelForm.action = `/projects/cancel-registration/${regId}`;

        const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('sheetCancelBtn');
        if (canCancel) {
            cancelForm.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
            cancelForm.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        const sheet = document.getElementById('regActionSheet');
        const content = document.getElementById('regActionContent');
        sheet.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeRegActionSheet() {
        const sheet = document.getElementById('regActionSheet');
        const content = document.getElementById('regActionContent');
        sheet.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    // 2. Şart Düzenleme Modal Fonksiyonları
    function openEditTermsModalFromButton(btn) {
        const collabId = btn.getAttribute('data-collab-id');
        const currentTerms = btn.getAttribute('data-terms') || '%50';
        const form = document.getElementById('editTermsForm');
        form.action = `/collaborations/update-terms/${collabId}`;
        document.getElementById('modalRateInput').value = currentTerms;
        const modal = document.getElementById('editTermsModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('editTermsContent');
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeEditTermsModal() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('editTermsModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('editTermsContent');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function setModalRate(val) {
        document.getElementById('modalRateInput').value = val;
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

                    <button type="button" onclick="setRating(1)" class="star-btn text-3xl text-amber-400 transition transform hover:scale-110">★</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setRating(2)" class="star-btn text-3xl text-amber-400 transition transform hover:scale-110">★</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setRating(3)" class="star-btn text-3xl text-amber-400 transition transform hover:scale-110">★</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setRating(4)" class="star-btn text-3xl text-amber-400 transition transform hover:scale-110">★</button>

                <textarea name="yorum" rows="3" placeholder="Süreç çok şeffaf ve hızlı ilerledi, tekrar çalışmak isterim..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none shadow-2xs leading-relaxed"></textarea>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>

    <!-- Gönder Butonu -->
    <div class="pt-4 pb-2">
        <button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('reviewForm').submit()" class="w-full bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-[0.99] text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm transition shadow-sm">
            Değerlendirmeyi Kaydet & Tamamla
        </button>
    </div>

<script>
    const labels = {
        1: "Çok Zayıf (1/5)",
        2: "Geliştirilmeli (2/5)",
        3: "Ortalama (3/5)",
        4: "Başarılı (4/5)",
        5: "Mükemmel (5/5)"
    };

    function setRating(val) {
        document.getElementById('selectedRating').value = val;
        document.getElementById('ratingLabel').innerText = labels[val];

        const stars = document.querySelectorAll('.star-btn');
        stars.forEach((s, idx) => {
            if (idx < val) {
                s.className = 'star-btn text-3xl text-amber-400 transition transform hover:scale-110';
            } else {
                s.className = 'star-btn text-3xl text-slate-200 transition transform hover:scale-110';
            }
        });
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

        </button>

<script>
    function openProjectDrawer() {
        document.getElementById('projectSearchInput').value = '';
        filterProjectList();

        const currentId = document.getElementById('selectedProjectId').value;
        document.querySelectorAll('.project-item-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const isSelected = btn.getAttribute('data-id') === currentId;
            if (isSelected) {
                btn.className = 'project-item-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between group shadow-2xs';
                btn.querySelector('span.opacity-0, span.opacity-100').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-100 font-black text-sm';
            } else {
                btn.className = 'project-item-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between group';
                btn.querySelector('span.opacity-100, span.opacity-0').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 font-black text-sm transition';
            }
        });

        const drawer = document.getElementById('projectPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('projectDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeProjectDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('projectPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('projectDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function selectProjectItem(btn) {
        const pId = btn.getAttribute('data-id');
        const pName = btn.getAttribute('data-name');
        const pRate = btn.getAttribute('data-rate');

        document.getElementById('selectedProjectId').value = pId;
        document.getElementById('displayProjectName').innerText = pName;
        document.getElementById('displayProjectRate').innerText = `Mevcut Komisyon: %${pRate}`;

        closeProjectDrawer();
    }

    function filterProjectList() {
        const query = document.getElementById('projectSearchInput').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
        document.querySelectorAll('.project-item-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const name = btn.getAttribute('data-name').toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
            if (name.includes(query)) {
                btn.style.display = 'flex';
            } else {
                btn.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

                        </svg>
                        <span id="bellUnreadBadge" class="hidden absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-rose-600 text-white text-[10px] font-black flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-white shadow-xs">
                            0

    <!-- Scriptler -->
    <script>
        function toggleCompanyProfileMenu() {
            const menu = document.getElementById('companyProfileDropdown');
            if (menu) menu.classList.toggle('hidden');
        }

        function toggleNotifDrawer() {
            const drawer = document.getElementById('notifDrawerDropdown');
            if (drawer) {
                drawer.classList.toggle('hidden');
                if (!drawer.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                    fetchNotifications();
                }
            }
        }

        async function fetchNotifications() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch('/company/api/notifications');
                const data = await res.json();
                
                const listEl = document.getElementById('drawerNotifList');
                const badgeEl = document.getElementById('bellUnreadBadge');
                const sidebarBadge = document.getElementById('sidebarNotifBadge');
                const drawerCount = document.getElementById('drawerUnreadCount');

                if (data.unread_count > 0) {
                    badgeEl.innerText = data.unread_count;
                    badgeEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                    sidebarBadge.innerText = data.unread_count;
                    sidebarBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
                    drawerCount.innerText = `${data.unread_count} Yeni`;
                } else {
                    badgeEl.classList.add('hidden');
                    sidebarBadge.classList.add('hidden');
                    drawerCount.innerText = '0 Yeni';
                }

                if (!data.notifications || data.notifications.length === 0) {
                    listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="py-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">Yeni bildiriminiz bulunmuyor.</div>';
                    return;
                }

                listEl.innerHTML = '';
                data.notifications.forEach(n => {
                    const item = document.createElement('a');
                    item.href = n.hedef_url || '/company/notifications';
                    item.className = `block p-2.5 rounded-2xl border transition ${n.okundu_mu ? 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-100 text-slate-600' : 'theme-bg-light border-slate-200 text-slate-900 font-bold'}`;
                    item.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 mb-0.5">
                            <span class="font-bold uppercase">${n.baslik}</span>
                            <span>${(n.created_at || '').substring(0, 10)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <p class="text-[11px] leading-snug line-clamp-2">${n.icerik}</p>
                    `;
                    listEl.appendChild(item);
                });

            } catch (e) {
                console.error("Bildirim çekme hatası:", e);
            }
        }

        async function markAllNotificationsAsRead() {
            try {
                await fetch('/company/api/notifications/read-all', { method: 'POST' });
                fetchNotifications();
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("Okundu işaretleme hatası:", e);
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            const profileMenu = document.getElementById('companyProfileDropdown');
            const notifDrawer = document.getElementById('notifDrawerDropdown');
            const bellBtn = document.getElementById('btnBellNotifications');

            if (profileMenu && !profileMenu.contains(e.target) && !e.target.closest('[onclick="toggleCompanyProfileMenu()"]')) {
                profileMenu.classList.add('hidden');
            }
            if (notifDrawer && !notifDrawer.contains(e.target) && !bellBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                notifDrawer.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        });

        // Sayfa açılışında yükleme ve her 15 saniyede bir canlı yoklama (Polling)
        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            fetchNotifications();
            setInterval(fetchNotifications, 15000);
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                <div class="flex items-start gap-3.5 min-w-0">

                {% if n.hedef_url %}

                </a>
                {% endif %}

<script>
    // 1. Seviye Çekmecesi
    function openLevelDrawer(partnerId, partnerName, currentLevel, currentStatus) {
        document.getElementById('drawerLevelPartnerId').value = partnerId;
        document.getElementById('levelDrawerPartnerName').innerText = partnerName;
        document.getElementById('levelDrawerCurrentStatus').value = currentStatus;
        document.getElementById('levelDrawerSelectedLevel').value = currentLevel;

        document.querySelectorAll('.level-option-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const isMatch = (btn.getAttribute('data-level') === currentLevel);
            if (isMatch) {
                btn.className = 'level-option-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between shadow-2xs';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-100 font-black text-sm';
            } else {
                btn.className = 'level-option-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-0 font-black text-sm';
            }
        });

        document.getElementById('levelDrawer').classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        document.getElementById('levelDrawerContent').classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeLevelDrawer() {
        document.getElementById('levelDrawer').classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        document.getElementById('levelDrawerContent').classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function selectLevelItem(level) {
        document.getElementById('levelDrawerSelectedLevel').value = level;
        document.querySelectorAll('.level-option-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const isMatch = (btn.getAttribute('data-level') === level);
            if (isMatch) {
                btn.className = 'level-option-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between shadow-2xs';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-100 font-black text-sm';
            } else {
                btn.className = 'level-option-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-0 font-black text-sm';
            }
        });
        document.getElementById('levelUpdateForm').submit();
    }

    // 2. Durum Çekmecesi
    function openStatusDrawer(partnerId, partnerName, currentStatus, currentLevel) {
        document.getElementById('statusDrawerPartnerId').value = partnerId;
        document.getElementById('statusDrawerPartnerName').innerText = partnerName;
        document.getElementById('statusDrawerCurrentLevel').value = currentLevel;
        document.getElementById('statusDrawerSelectedStatus').value = currentStatus;

        document.querySelectorAll('.partner-status-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const isMatch = (btn.getAttribute('data-status') === currentStatus);
            if (isMatch) {
                btn.className = 'partner-status-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between shadow-2xs';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-100 font-black text-sm';
            } else {
                btn.className = 'partner-status-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-0 font-black text-sm';
            }
        });

        document.getElementById('statusDrawer').classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        document.getElementById('statusDrawerContent').classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeStatusDrawer() {
        document.getElementById('statusDrawer').classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        document.getElementById('statusDrawerContent').classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function selectStatusItem(status) {
        document.getElementById('statusDrawerSelectedStatus').value = status;
        document.querySelectorAll('.partner-status-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const isMatch = (btn.getAttribute('data-status') === status);
            if (isMatch) {
                btn.className = 'partner-status-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between shadow-2xs';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-100 font-black text-sm';
            } else {
                btn.className = 'partner-status-btn w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-primary opacity-0 font-black text-sm';
            }
        });
        document.getElementById('statusUpdateForm').submit();
    }

    // 3. Canlı Mesajlaşma Konsolu (Slide-over Flyout)
    let chatPollInterval = null;
    let activeChatUserId = null;

    function openChatFlyout(userId, name, office, photo) {
        activeChatUserId = userId;
        document.getElementById('flyoutTargetUserId').value = userId;
        document.getElementById('flyoutName').innerText = name;
        document.getElementById('flyoutOffice').innerText = office;

        const av = document.getElementById('flyoutAvatar');
        if (photo && photo !== 'None' && photo !== '') {
            av.innerHTML = `<img src="${photo}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">`;
        } else {
            av.innerText = (name.substring(0, 1)).toUpperCase();
        }

        const overlay = document.getElementById('chatFlyoutOverlay');
        const flyout = document.getElementById('chatFlyout');

        overlay.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        flyout.classList.remove('translate-x-full');

        loadFlyoutMessages(userId);

        if (chatPollInterval) clearInterval(chatPollInterval);
        chatPollInterval = setInterval(() => {
            if (activeChatUserId) pollFlyoutMessages(activeChatUserId);
        }, 3000);
    }

    function closeChatFlyout() {
        activeChatUserId = null;
        if (chatPollInterval) clearInterval(chatPollInterval);

        const overlay = document.getElementById('chatFlyoutOverlay');
        const flyout = document.getElementById('chatFlyout');

        overlay.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        flyout.classList.add('translate-x-full');
    }

    async function loadFlyoutMessages(userId) {
        const list = document.getElementById('flyoutMessagesList');
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="py-12 text-center text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px]">Mesajlar yükleniyor...</div>';
        
        await pollFlyoutMessages(userId, true);
    }

    async function pollFlyoutMessages(userId, autoScroll = false) {
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/messages/api/poll/${userId}`);
            if (!res.ok) return;
            const data = await res.json();
            const msgs = data.messages || [];
            const myId = data.current_user_id;

            const list = document.getElementById('flyoutMessagesList');

            if (msgs.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML = '<div class="py-12 text-center text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px]">Henüz bir mesajlaşma bulunmuyor.<br>İlk mesajı aşağıdan iletebilirsiniz.</div>';
                return;
            }

            let html = '';
            msgs.forEach(m => {
                const isMe = (m.gonderen_id === myId);
                const timeStr = m.created_at ? m.created_at.substring(11, 16) : '';
                
                if (isMe) {
                    html += `
                    <div class="flex flex-col items-end">
                        <div class="theme-bg-primary text-white rounded-2xl rounded-tr-xs px-3.5 py-2 max-w-[80%] shadow-xs leading-relaxed break-words">
                            ${m.mesaj_metni}
                        </div>
                        <span class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 mt-1 font-mono">${timeStr}</span>
                    </div>`;
                } else {
                    html += `
                    <div class="flex flex-col items-start">
                        <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-xs px-3.5 py-2 max-w-[80%] shadow-xs leading-relaxed break-words">
                            ${m.mesaj_metni}
                        </div>
                        <span class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 mt-1 font-mono">${timeStr}</span>
                    </div>`;
                }
            });

            list.innerHTML = html;

            if (autoScroll) {
                list.scrollTop = list.scrollHeight;
            }
        } catch(e) {
            console.error("Chat polling hatası:", e);
        }
    }

    async function handleSendFlyoutMessage(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const input = document.getElementById('flyoutMessageInput');
        const receiverId = document.getElementById('flyoutTargetUserId').value;
        const msgText = input.value.trim();

        if (!msgText || !receiverId) return;

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('receiver_id', receiverId);
        formData.append('message', msgText);

        input.value = '';

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/messages/api/send', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            });

            if (res.ok) {
                pollFlyoutMessages(receiverId, true);
            }
        } catch(err) {
            console.error("Mesaj iletilemedi:", err);
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function handleLogoFileSelect(input) {
        const display = document.getElementById('logoFileNameDisplay');
        if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
            display.innerText = input.files[0].name;
            display.className = 'text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate max-w-[150px]';
        } else {
            display.innerText = 'Seçilmedi';
            display.className = 'text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium truncate max-w-[150px]';
        }
    }

    const colorThemes = [
        { name: "Zümrüt", primary: "#059669", dark: "#047857", light: "#ecfdf5", border: "#a7f3d0" },
        { name: "Safir Mavi", primary: "#2563eb", dark: "#1d4ed8", light: "#eff6ff", border: "#bfdbfe" },
        { name: "Lüks Altın", primary: "#d97706", dark: "#b45309", light: "#fffbeb", border: "#fde68a" },
        { name: "Gece Moru", primary: "#7c3aed", dark: "#6d28d9", light: "#f5f3ff", border: "#ddd6fe" },
        { name: "Okyanus Turkuaz", primary: "#0891b2", dark: "#0e7490", light: "#ecfeff", border: "#a5f3fc" },
        { name: "Kraliyet Bordo", primary: "#e11d48", dark: "#be123c", light: "#fff1f2", border: "#fecdd3" },
        { name: "Derin Çivit", primary: "#4f46e5", dark: "#4338ca", light: "#eef2ff", border: "#c7d2fe" },
        { name: "Bakır Bronz", primary: "#ea580c", dark: "#c2410c", light: "#fff7ed", border: "#fed7aa" },
        { name: "Orman Yeşili", primary: "#16a34a", dark: "#15803d", light: "#f0fdf4", border: "#bbf7d0" },
        { name: "Füme Grafit", primary: "#475569", dark: "#334155", light: "#f8fafc", border: "#cbd5e1" },
        { name: "Gök Mavisi", primary: "#0284c7", dark: "#0369a1", light: "#f0f9ff", border: "#bae6fd" },
        { name: "Gül Kurusu", primary: "#db2777", dark: "#be185d", light: "#fdf2f8", border: "#fbcfe8" },
        { name: "Zeytin Haki", primary: "#65a30d", dark: "#4d7c0f", light: "#f7fee7", border: "#d9f99d" },
        { name: "Asil Amber", primary: "#f59e0b", dark: "#d97706", light: "#fffbeb", border: "#fde68a" },
        { name: "Mercan", primary: "#f43f5e", dark: "#e11d48", light: "#fff1f2", border: "#fecdd3" },
        { name: "Koyu Petrol", primary: "#0f766e", dark: "#115e59", light: "#f0fdfa", border: "#99f6e4" }
    ];

    function applyTheme(theme) {
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-primary', theme.primary);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-dark', theme.dark);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-light', theme.light);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-border', theme.border);

        localStorage.setItem('company_theme_color', theme.primary);
        localStorage.setItem('company_theme_dark', theme.dark);
        localStorage.setItem('company_theme_light', theme.light);
        localStorage.setItem('company_theme_border', theme.border);
        localStorage.setItem('company_theme_name', theme.name);

        document.getElementById('themeNameBadge').innerText = theme.name;
        renderPalette();
    }

    function resetDefaultTheme() {
        applyTheme(colorThemes[0]);
    }

    function renderPalette() {
        const container = document.getElementById('colorPaletteContainer');
        if (!container) return;

        const currentPrimary = localStorage.getItem('company_theme_color') || "#059669";
        const currentName = localStorage.getItem('company_theme_name') || "Zümrüt";
        document.getElementById('themeNameBadge').innerText = currentName;

        container.innerHTML = '';

        colorThemes.forEach(t => {
            const isSelected = (t.primary.toLowerCase() === currentPrimary.toLowerCase());
            const btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.title = t.name;
            btn.onclick = () => applyTheme(t);
            btn.className = `h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-150 transform hover:scale-105 ${isSelected ? 'ring-2 ring-slate-900 ring-offset-2 scale-105 shadow-sm' : 'border border-black/10'}`;
            btn.style.backgroundColor = t.primary;

            if (isSelected) {
                btn.innerHTML = `<span class="text-white text-xs font-black drop-shadow-xs">✓</span>`;
            }

            container.appendChild(btn);
        });
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        renderPalette();
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function openProjectQuickDetail(card) {
        const id = card.getAttribute('data-id');
        const ad = card.getAttribute('data-ad');
        const tip = card.getAttribute('data-tip');
        const il = card.getAttribute('data-il');
        const ilce = card.getAttribute('data-ilce');
        const bolge = card.getAttribute('data-bolge');
        const durum = card.getAttribute('data-durum');
        const minF = Number(card.getAttribute('data-min') || 0);
        const maxF = Number(card.getAttribute('data-max') || 0);
        const kom = card.getAttribute('data-komisyon');
        const vipKom = card.getAttribute('data-vipkomisyon');
        const stok = card.getAttribute('data-stok');
        const toplam = card.getAttribute('data-toplam');
        const teslim = card.getAttribute('data-teslim');
        const aciklama = card.getAttribute('data-aciklama');
        const odeme = card.getAttribute('data-odeme');
        const kapak = card.getAttribute('data-kapak');
        const brosur = card.getAttribute('data-brosur');

        let galeri = [];
        try {
            galeri = JSON.parse(card.getAttribute('data-galeri') || '[]');
        } catch(e) { galeri = []; }

        document.getElementById('modalProjType').innerText = tip;
        document.getElementById('modalProjTitle').innerText = ad;
        document.getElementById('modalProjStatus').innerText = durum;
        document.getElementById('modalProjCover').src = kapak;
        
        document.getElementById('modalProjPrice').innerText = `${minF.toLocaleString('tr-TR')} - ${maxF.toLocaleString('tr-TR')} TL`;
        document.getElementById('modalProjCommissions').innerText = `Standart: %${kom} | VIP: %${vipKom}`;
        
        document.getElementById('modalProjLocation').innerText = `${ilce}, ${il}` + (bolge ? ` (${bolge})` : '');
        document.getElementById('modalProjStock').innerText = `${stok} Ünite`;
        document.getElementById('modalProjTotal').innerText = `${toplam} Ünite`;
        document.getElementById('modalProjDelivery').innerText = teslim;
        
        document.getElementById('modalProjDesc').innerText = aciklama || 'Proje hakkında detaylı açıklama girilmemiş.';
        
        const payBox = document.getElementById('modalProjPaymentBox');
        if (odeme && odeme.trim()) {
            payBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modalProjPayment').innerText = odeme;
        } else {
            payBox.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        const brochBox = document.getElementById('modalBrochureBox');
        if (brosur && brosur.trim()) {
            brochBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modalBrochureLink').href = brosur;
        } else {
            brochBox.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        // Galeri Küçük Resimlerini Doldur
        const galContainer = document.getElementById('modalProjGalleryList');
        galContainer.innerHTML = '';
        if (galeri && galeri.length > 0) {
            galeri.forEach(gUrl => {
                const imgThumb = document.createElement('img');
                imgThumb.src = gUrl;
                imgThumb.className = "w-16 h-12 rounded-xl object-cover border border-slate-200 shrink-0 cursor-pointer hover:opacity-80 transition";
                imgThumb.onclick = () => { document.getElementById('modalProjCover').src = gUrl; };
                galContainer.appendChild(imgThumb);
            });
        }

        document.getElementById('modalOpenDetailLink').href = `/projects/detail/${id}`;
        document.getElementById('modalEditBtn').href = `/company/projects/edit/${id}`;

        const modal = document.getElementById('projectDetailModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('projectDetailContent');
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('scale-95');
    }

    function closeProjectQuickDetail() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('projectDetailModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('projectDetailContent');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('scale-95');
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

======================================================================

<div id="locationPickerDrawer" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-end justify-center opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-200">
    <div id="locationDrawerContent" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-3.5 shadow-2xl border-t theme-border-primary transform translate-y-full transition-transform duration-300 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-between">

<script id="turkeyDataJson" type="application/json">

</script>

<script>
    // Daire Tipleri Çoklu Seçimi
    let selectedRoomTypes = [];

    function toggleRoomTypeTag(typeName, btn) {
        const idx = selectedRoomTypes.indexOf(typeName);
        if (idx > -1) {
            selectedRoomTypes.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = "room-type-chip px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 transition";
            btn.innerText = "+ " + typeName;
        } else {
            selectedRoomTypes.push(typeName);
            btn.className = "room-type-chip px-3 py-1.5 theme-bg-primary text-white border theme-border-primary rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition shadow-2xs";
            btn.innerText = "✓ " + typeName;
        }

        const container = document.getElementById('hiddenRoomTypesContainer');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        selectedRoomTypes.forEach(t => {
            const input = document.createElement('input');
            input.type = 'hidden';
            input.name = 'daire_tipleri';
            input.value = t;
            container.appendChild(input);
        });
    }

    function updateFileLabel(input, textId) {
        const textEl = document.getElementById(textId);
        if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
            textEl.innerText = input.files[0].name;
            textEl.className = 'text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 truncate';
        } else {
            textEl.innerText = 'Seçilmedi';
            textEl.className = 'text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-medium truncate';
        }
    }

    function updateMultiFileLabel(input, textId) {
        const textEl = document.getElementById(textId);
        if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
            textEl.innerText = `${input.files.length} dosya seçildi`;
            textEl.className = 'text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 truncate';
        } else {
            textEl.innerText = 'Seçilmedi';
            textEl.className = 'text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-medium truncate';
        }
    }

    let selectedFeaturesList = [];

    function toggleFeatureTag(featureName, btn) {
        const idx = selectedFeaturesList.indexOf(featureName);
        if (idx > -1) {
            selectedFeaturesList.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = "feature-chip px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 transition";
            btn.innerText = "+ " + featureName;
        } else {
            selectedFeaturesList.push(featureName);
            btn.className = "feature-chip px-2.5 py-1 theme-bg-primary text-white border theme-border-primary rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black transition shadow-2xs";
            btn.innerText = "✓ " + featureName;
        }

        document.getElementById('featureCountBadge').innerText = `${selectedFeaturesList.length} Seçildi`;
        
        const descArea = document.getElementById('projectDescriptionInput');
        let currentText = descArea.value;
        const parts = currentText.split("\n\nÖzellikler:");
        const baseDesc = parts[0].trim();
        
        if (selectedFeaturesList.length > 0) {
            descArea.value = baseDesc ? `${baseDesc}\n\nÖzellikler: ${selectedFeaturesList.join(', ')}` : selectedFeaturesList.join(', ');
        } else {
            descArea.value = baseDesc;
        }
    }

    function formatPriceField(input, hiddenId) {
        let rawVal = input.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
        document.getElementById(hiddenId).value = rawVal || '0';
        if (rawVal) {
            input.value = Number(rawVal).toLocaleString('tr-TR');
        } else {
            input.value = '';
        }
    }

    const rawDataEl = document.getElementById('turkeyDataJson');
    let turkeyData = {};
    try {
        turkeyData = JSON.parse(rawDataEl.textContent || '{}');
    } catch(e) {
        turkeyData = { "İstanbul": ["Beşiktaş", "Beykoz", "Kadıköy", "Sarıyer"] };
    }

    let currentPickerMode = 'city';
    let currentPool = [];

    function openLocationDrawer(mode) {
        currentPickerMode = mode;
        const titleEl = document.getElementById('locationDrawerTitle');
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput');
        searchInput.value = '';

        const collator = new Intl.Collator('tr');

        if (mode === 'city') {
            titleEl.innerText = 'İl Seçiniz';
            currentPool = Object.keys(turkeyData).sort(collator.compare);
        } else {
            const selectedCity = document.getElementById('realCityInput').value || 'İstanbul';
            titleEl.innerText = `${selectedCity} - İlçe Seçiniz`;
            const rawDistricts = turkeyData[selectedCity] || ["Merkez"];
            currentPool = [...rawDistricts].sort(collator.compare);
        }

        renderDrawerOptions(currentPool);

        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeLocationDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function renderDrawerOptions(items) {
        const container = document.getElementById('drawerOptionsList');
        container.innerHTML = '';

        const currentActive = (currentPickerMode === 'city') 
            ? document.getElementById('realCityInput').value 
            : document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value;

        if (!items || items.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">Eşleşen sonuç bulunamadı.</div>';
            return;
        }

        items.forEach(item => {
            const isSelected = (item.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr') === currentActive.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'));
            const optBtn = document.createElement('button');
            optBtn.type = 'button';
            optBtn.onclick = () => selectLocationItem(item);

            if (isSelected) {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl theme-bg-primary text-white font-black text-xs transition shadow-xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span><span class="text-sm">✓</span>`;
            } else {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-slate-800 hover:theme-text-dark border border-slate-200/80 font-bold text-xs transition shadow-2xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span>`;
            }

            container.appendChild(optBtn);
        });
    }

    function filterDrawerOptions() {
        const query = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
        const filtered = currentPool.filter(it => it.toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(query));
        renderDrawerOptions(filtered);
    }

    function selectLocationItem(val) {
        if (currentPickerMode === 'city') {
            document.getElementById('realCityInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayCityText').innerText = val;

            const availableDistricts = turkeyData[val] || ["Merkez"];
            const defaultDistrict = availableDistricts[0];
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = defaultDistrict;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = defaultDistrict;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = val;
        }
        closeLocationDrawer();
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<div id="locationPickerDrawer" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-end justify-center opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-200">
    <div id="locationDrawerContent" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-3.5 shadow-2xl border-t theme-border-primary transform translate-y-full transition-transform duration-300 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-between">

                <button type="button" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-lg font-bold">✕</button>
            </div>
            
            <div class="mt-2.5">
                <input type="text" id="drawerSearchInput" oninput="filterDrawerOptions()" placeholder="Listede ara..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 shadow-2xs">
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="drawerOptionsList" class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 pr-1 py-1 custom-scrollbar"></div>

        <button type="button" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="w-full py-3 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-2xl text-xs transition mt-2">
            Vazgeç

<script id="turkeyDataJson" type="application/json">
{{ turkey_data|tojson if turkey_data else '{}' }}
</script>

<script>
    // 1. 40 Nitelik Çipi Seçim Motoru ve Mevcut Açıklamadan Yükleme
    let selectedFeaturesList = [];

    function initExistingFeatures() {
        const descArea = document.getElementById('projectDescriptionInput');
        const rawDesc = descArea ? descArea.value.toLowerCase() : '';

        document.querySelectorAll('#featuresGridContainer button[data-feat]').forEach(btn => {
            const fName = btn.getAttribute('data-feat');
            if (rawDesc.includes(fName.toLowerCase())) {
                selectedFeaturesList.push(fName);
                btn.className = "feature-chip px-2.5 py-1 theme-bg-primary text-white border theme-border-primary rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black transition shadow-2xs";
                btn.innerText = "✓ " + fName;
            }
        });

        document.getElementById('featureCountBadge').innerText = `${selectedFeaturesList.length} Seçildi`;
    }

    function toggleFeatureTag(featureName, btn) {
        const idx = selectedFeaturesList.indexOf(featureName);
        if (idx > -1) {
            selectedFeaturesList.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = "feature-chip px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 transition";
            btn.innerText = "+ " + featureName;
        } else {
            selectedFeaturesList.push(featureName);
            btn.className = "feature-chip px-2.5 py-1 theme-bg-primary text-white border theme-border-primary rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black transition shadow-2xs";
            btn.innerText = "✓ " + featureName;
        }

        document.getElementById('featureCountBadge').innerText = `${selectedFeaturesList.length} Seçildi`;
        
        const descArea = document.getElementById('projectDescriptionInput');
        let currentText = descArea.value;
        const parts = currentText.split("\n\nÖzellikler:");
        const baseDesc = parts[0].trim();
        
        if (selectedFeaturesList.length > 0) {
            descArea.value = baseDesc ? `${baseDesc}\n\nÖzellikler: ${selectedFeaturesList.join(', ')}` : selectedFeaturesList.join(', ');
        } else {
            descArea.value = baseDesc;
        }
    }

    // 2. Fiyat Formatlama
    function formatPriceField(input, hiddenId) {
        let rawVal = input.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
        document.getElementById(hiddenId).value = rawVal || '0';
        if (rawVal) {
            input.value = Number(rawVal).toLocaleString('tr-TR');
        } else {
            input.value = '';
        }
    }

    // 3. Konum Çekmecesi
    const rawDataEl = document.getElementById('turkeyDataJson');
    let turkeyData = {};
    try {
        turkeyData = JSON.parse(rawDataEl.textContent || '{}');
    } catch(e) {
        turkeyData = { "İstanbul": ["Beşiktaş", "Beykoz", "Kadıköy", "Sarıyer"] };
    }

    let currentPickerMode = 'city';
    let currentPool = [];

    function openLocationDrawer(mode) {
        currentPickerMode = mode;
        const titleEl = document.getElementById('locationDrawerTitle');
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput');
        searchInput.value = '';

        const collator = new Intl.Collator('tr');

        if (mode === 'city') {
            titleEl.innerText = 'İl Seçiniz';
            currentPool = Object.keys(turkeyData).sort(collator.compare);
        } else {
            const selectedCity = document.getElementById('realCityInput').value || 'İstanbul';
            titleEl.innerText = `${selectedCity} - İlçe Seçiniz`;
            const rawDistricts = turkeyData[selectedCity] || ["Merkez"];
            currentPool = [...rawDistricts].sort(collator.compare);
        }

        renderDrawerOptions(currentPool);

        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeLocationDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function renderDrawerOptions(items) {
        const container = document.getElementById('drawerOptionsList');
        container.innerHTML = '';

        const currentActive = (currentPickerMode === 'city') 
            ? document.getElementById('realCityInput').value 
            : document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value;

        if (!items || items.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">Eşleşen sonuç bulunamadı.</div>';
            return;
        }

        items.forEach(item => {
            const isSelected = (item.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr') === currentActive.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'));
            const optBtn = document.createElement('button');
            optBtn.type = 'button';
            optBtn.onclick = () => selectLocationItem(item);

            if (isSelected) {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl theme-bg-primary text-white font-black text-xs transition shadow-xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span><span class="text-sm">✓</span>`;
            } else {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-slate-800 hover:theme-text-dark border border-slate-200/80 font-bold text-xs transition shadow-2xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span>`;
            }

            container.appendChild(optBtn);
        });
    }

    function filterDrawerOptions() {
        const query = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
        const filtered = currentPool.filter(it => it.toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(query));
        renderDrawerOptions(filtered);
    }

    function selectLocationItem(val) {
        if (currentPickerMode === 'city') {
            document.getElementById('realCityInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayCityText').innerText = val;

            const availableDistricts = turkeyData[val] || ["Merkez"];
            const defaultDistrict = availableDistricts[0];
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = defaultDistrict;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = defaultDistrict;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = val;
        }
        closeLocationDrawer();
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        initExistingFeatures();
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

                        <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 block uppercase">Maskeli Telefon:</span>
                        <span class="font-mono font-extrabold text-slate-700 block mt-0.5">{{ r.musteri_telefon_maskeli or r.musteri_telefon }}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 block uppercase">Koruma Bitiş Tarihi:</span>
                        <span class="font-mono font-black theme-text-dark block mt-0.5">{{ r.koruma_bitis_tarihi[:10] if r.koruma_bitis_tarihi else '-' }}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Durum Güncelleme ve Buton Alanı -->
                {% if not is_project_inactive %}
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                    <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500">Müşterinin görüşme ve süreç aşamasını güncelleyin:</span>
                    
                    <button type="button" 
                            onclick="openRegistrationStatusDrawer('{{ r.id }}', '{{ r.musteri_ad_soyad }}', '{{ r.durum }}')"
                            class="px-4 py-2 theme-bg-light hover:theme-bg-primary hover:text-white theme-text-dark border theme-border-light text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition shadow-2xs flex items-center gap-1.5 active:scale-95">
                        <span>Aşamayı Değiştir: <strong>{{ r.durum }}</strong></span>
                        <span class="text-xs">▾</span>
                    </button>
                </div>
                {% else %}
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1 text-xs">
                    <span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-700 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <span>⚠️ Bu proje yayından kaldırıldığı için müşteri tescil durumu güncellenemez.</span>
                    </span>
                    <span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 font-bold">Kilitli</span>
                </div>
                {% endif %}

            </div>
            {% endfor %}

            {% if total_pages > 1 %}
            <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 pt-6">
                {% if page > 1 %}
                <a href="/company/registrations?page={{ page - 1 }}" class="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl transition shadow-2xs">
                    ‹ Önceki
                </a>
                {% endif %}

                {% for p_num in range(1, total_pages + 1) %}
                <a href="/company/registrations?page={{ p_num }}" class="w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition shadow-2xs {% if p_num == page %}theme-bg-primary text-white{% else %}bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50{% endif %}">
                    {{ p_num }}
                </a>
                {% endfor %}

                {% if page < total_pages %}
                <a href="/company/registrations?page={{ page + 1 }}" class="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl transition shadow-2xs">
                    Sonraki ›
                </a>
                {% endif %}
            </div>
            {% endif %}

                <h3 class="text-xs font-black text-slate-700">Henüz Kayıtlı Müşteri Tescili Bulunmuyor</h3>
                <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto">Danışmanlar projelerinize hazır alıcı tescilledikçe bu listede 90 gün korumalı olarak takip edebilirsiniz.</p>
            </div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

            <input type="hidden" name="yeni_durum" id="drawerNewStatusInput" value="">

                {% for st_item in ["Doğrulandı", "Görüşmede", "Yer Gösterimi", "Rezervasyon", "Satış Yapıldı", "İptal Edildi"] %}
                <button type="button" 
                        data-status="{{ st_item }}"
                        onclick="selectRegistrationStatus('{{ st_item }}')"
                        class="status-option-btn w-full p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light hover:theme-border-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between group">
                    <span class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-800 group-hover:theme-text-dark">{{ st_item }}</span>
                    <span class="theme-text-dark opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 font-black text-sm transition">✓</span>
                </button>
                {% endfor %}
            </div>

                <button type="submit" class="w-full py-3 theme-bg-primary theme-hover-bg text-white font-black text-xs rounded-2xl transition shadow-xs active:scale-95">

                </button>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>

<script>
    function openRegistrationStatusDrawer(regId, customerName, currentStatus) {
        document.getElementById('drawerRegId').value = regId;
        document.getElementById('drawerCustomerName').innerText = customerName;
        document.getElementById('drawerNewStatusInput').value = currentStatus;

        document.querySelectorAll('.status-option-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const st = btn.getAttribute('data-status');
            const isMatch = (st.toLowerCase() === currentStatus.toLowerCase());
            if (isMatch) {
                btn.className = 'status-option-btn w-full p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between group shadow-2xs';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-dark opacity-100 font-black text-sm';
            } else {
                btn.className = 'status-option-btn w-full p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between group';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-dark opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 font-black text-sm transition';
            }
        });

        const drawer = document.getElementById('statusDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('statusDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeRegistrationStatusDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('statusDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('statusDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function selectRegistrationStatus(statusName) {
        document.getElementById('drawerNewStatusInput').value = statusName;

        document.querySelectorAll('.status-option-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const st = btn.getAttribute('data-status');
            if (st === statusName) {
                btn.className = 'status-option-btn w-full p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between group shadow-2xs';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-dark opacity-100 font-black text-sm';
            } else {
                btn.className = 'status-option-btn w-full p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-left transition flex items-center justify-between group';
                btn.querySelector('span:last-child').className = 'theme-text-dark opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 font-black text-sm transition';
            }
        });
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function openFinalizeModalFromBtn(btn) {
        const regId = btn.getAttribute('data-reg-id');
        const custName = btn.getAttribute('data-customer');
        const projName = btn.getAttribute('data-project');

        document.getElementById('modalRegId').value = regId;
        document.getElementById('modalCustomerName').innerText = custName;
        document.getElementById('modalProjectName').innerText = projName;
        document.getElementById('finalizeModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeFinalizeModal() {
        document.getElementById('finalizeModal').classList.add('hidden');
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    async function refreshUnreadBadge() {
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/notifications/api/unread-count');
            if (!res.ok) return;
            const data = await res.json();
            const badge = document.getElementById('agentBellBadge');
            if (badge && data && typeof data.unread_count === 'number') {
                if (data.unread_count > 0) {
                    badge.innerText = data.unread_count;
                    badge.classList.remove('hidden');
                    badge.classList.add('flex');
                } else {
                    badge.classList.add('hidden');
                    badge.classList.remove('flex');
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("Bildirim sayısı kontrol hatası:", e);
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        // Sayfa ilk yüklendiğinde sunucunun verdiği unread_notifications korunur,
        // periyodik kontrol her 15 saniyede bir arka planda devam eder.
        setInterval(refreshUnreadBadge, 15000);
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

<div id="locationPickerDrawer" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-end justify-center opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-200">
    <div id="locationDrawerContent" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-3.5 shadow-2xl border-t theme-border-primary transform translate-y-full transition-transform duration-300 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-between">
        
        <div class="w-12 h-1 bg-slate-300 rounded-full mx-auto"></div>

            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100">
                <h3 id="locationDrawerTitle" class="text-xs font-black theme-text-dark uppercase tracking-tight">İl Seçiniz</h3>
                <button type="button" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-lg font-bold">✕</button>
            </div>
            
            <div class="mt-2.5">
                <input type="text" id="drawerSearchInput" oninput="filterDrawerOptions()" placeholder="Listede ara..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:theme-border-primary shadow-2xs">
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="drawerOptionsList" class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 pr-1 py-1 custom-scrollbar"></div>

        <button type="button" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="w-full py-3 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-2xl text-xs transition mt-2">
            Vazgeç
        </button>
    </div>

<script id="turkeyDataJson" type="application/json">
{{ turkey_data|tojson if turkey_data else '{}' }}
</script>

<script>
    const rawDataEl = document.getElementById('turkeyDataJson');
    let turkeyData = {};
    try {
        turkeyData = JSON.parse(rawDataEl.textContent || '{}');
    } catch(e) {
        turkeyData = { "İstanbul": ["Beşiktaş", "Beykoz", "Kadıköy", "Sarıyer"] };
    }

    let currentPickerMode = 'city';
    let currentPool = [];

    function openLocationDrawer(mode) {
        currentPickerMode = mode;
        const titleEl = document.getElementById('locationDrawerTitle');
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput');
        searchInput.value = '';

        const collator = new Intl.Collator('tr');

        if (mode === 'city') {
            titleEl.innerText = 'İl Seçiniz';
            currentPool = Object.keys(turkeyData).sort(collator.compare);
        } else {
            const selectedCity = document.getElementById('realCityInput').value || 'İstanbul';
            titleEl.innerText = `${selectedCity} - İlçe Seçiniz`;
            const rawDistricts = turkeyData[selectedCity] || ["Merkez"];
            currentPool = [...rawDistricts].sort(collator.compare);
        }

        renderDrawerOptions(currentPool);

        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeLocationDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function renderDrawerOptions(items) {
        const container = document.getElementById('drawerOptionsList');
        container.innerHTML = '';

        const currentActive = (currentPickerMode === 'city') 
            ? document.getElementById('realCityInput').value 
            : document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value;

        if (!items || items.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">Eşleşen sonuç bulunamadı.</div>';
            return;
        }

        items.forEach(item => {
            const isSelected = (item.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr') === currentActive.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'));
            const optBtn = document.createElement('button');
            optBtn.type = 'button';
            optBtn.onclick = () => selectLocationItem(item);

            if (isSelected) {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl theme-bg-primary text-white font-black text-xs transition shadow-xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span><span class="text-sm">✓</span>`;
            } else {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-slate-800 hover:theme-text-dark border border-slate-200/80 font-bold text-xs transition shadow-2xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span>`;
            }

            container.appendChild(optBtn);
        });
    }

    function filterDrawerOptions() {
        const query = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
        const filtered = currentPool.filter(it => it.toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(query));
        renderDrawerOptions(filtered);
    }

    function selectLocationItem(val) {
        if (currentPickerMode === 'city') {
            document.getElementById('realCityInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayCityText').innerText = val;

            const availableDistricts = turkeyData[val] || ["Merkez"];
            const defaultDistrict = availableDistricts[0];
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = defaultDistrict;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = defaultDistrict;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = val;
        }
        closeLocationDrawer();
    }

    function selectType(typeName, btn) {
        document.getElementById('selectedType').value = typeName;
        document.querySelectorAll('.type-btn').forEach(b => {
            b.className = 'type-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:theme-border-primary shadow-2xs transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ' + (b.classList.contains('col-span-2') ? 'col-span-2' : '');
        });
        btn.className = 'type-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light theme-text-dark font-extrabold shadow-xs transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ' + (btn.classList.contains('col-span-2') ? 'col-span-2' : '');
    }

    function selectCustomerStatus(statusName, btn) {
        document.getElementById('selectedCustomerStatus').value = statusName;
        document.querySelectorAll('.status-btn').forEach(b => {
            b.className = 'status-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:theme-border-primary shadow-2xs transition-all';
        });
        btn.className = 'status-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light theme-text-dark font-extrabold shadow-xs transition-all';
    }

    function selectDemandVisibility(visType, card) {
        document.getElementById('selectedVisibility').value = visType;
        document.querySelectorAll('.demand-vis-card').forEach(c => {
            c.className = 'demand-vis-card flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-50 transition shadow-2xs';
            const circle = c.querySelector('.radio-circle');
            const dot = c.querySelector('.dot');
            circle.className = 'w-4 h-4 rounded-full border border-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center radio-circle';
            dot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent dot';
        });

        card.className = 'demand-vis-card flex items-center justify-between p-3 theme-bg-light border-2 theme-border-primary rounded-2xl cursor-pointer transition shadow-2xs';
        const circle = card.querySelector('.radio-circle');
        const dot = card.querySelector('.dot');
        circle.className = 'w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 theme-border-primary flex items-center justify-center radio-circle';
        dot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full theme-bg-primary dot';
    }

    function formatPriceField(input, hiddenId) {
        let rawVal = input.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
        document.getElementById(hiddenId).value = rawVal || '0';
        if (rawVal) {
            input.value = Number(rawVal).toLocaleString('tr-TR');
        } else {
            input.value = '';
        }
    }

    let selectedFeatures = [];

    function toggleFeatureChip(feat, btn) {
        const idx = selectedFeatures.indexOf(feat);
        if (idx > -1) {
            selectedFeatures.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = 'demand-chip px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-semibold border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:theme-border-primary transition';
            btn.innerText = `+ ${feat}`;
        } else {
            selectedFeatures.push(feat);
            btn.className = 'demand-chip px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light theme-text-dark transition shadow-2xs';
            btn.innerText = `✓ ${feat}`;
        }

        const container = document.getElementById('hiddenFeaturesContainer');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        selectedFeatures.forEach(f => {
            const hiddenInput = document.createElement('input');
            hiddenInput.type = 'hidden';
            hiddenInput.name = 'ozellikler';
            hiddenInput.value = f;
            container.appendChild(hiddenInput);
        });

        document.getElementById('selectedFeatureBadge').innerText = `${selectedFeatures.length} seçildi`;
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

======================================================================

<script>
    function switchResultTab(tabName) {
        const btnProjects = document.getElementById('btnTabProjects');
        const btnPortfolios = document.getElementById('btnTabPortfolios');
        const secProjects = document.getElementById('sectionProjects');
        const secPortfolios = document.getElementById('sectionPortfolios');

        if (tabName === 'projects') {
            btnProjects.className = 'py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all duration-150 theme-bg-primary text-white shadow-sm';
            btnPortfolios.className = 'py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all duration-150 theme-text-dark hover:bg-white/50';
            secProjects.classList.remove('hidden');
            secPortfolios.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            btnPortfolios.className = 'py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all duration-150 theme-bg-primary text-white shadow-sm';
            btnProjects.className = 'py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all duration-150 theme-text-dark hover:bg-white/50';
            secPortfolios.classList.remove('hidden');
            secProjects.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}
