{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block title %}{{ other_user.ad_soyad or 'Sohbet' }} - EMLAKMATCH{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<!-- Ana Kapsayıcı: is_blocked verisini HTML attribute olarak taşıyoruz -->
<div id="chatRoot" 
     data-current-user-id="{{ current_user_id }}"
     data-other-user-id="{{ other_user.id }}"
     data-collab-id="{{ collab_id or '' }}"
     data-is-blocked="{% if is_blocked %}true{% else %}false{% endif %}"
     class="max-w-md mx-auto h-[calc(100vh-100px)] flex flex-col justify-between bg-slate-50/50">
    
    <!-- Üst Bar (Dinamik Geri Dönüşlü & Kurumsal Temalı) -->
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 bg-white border-b border-slate-100 shrink-0 shadow-2xs">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
            <button type="button" onclick="if(document.referrer){history.back();}else{window.location.href='{% if collab_id %}/collaborations/detail/{{ collab_id }}{% else %}/messages{% endif %}';}" class="p-1 -ml-1 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 transition">
                <svg class="w-6 h-6 stroke-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5">
                    <path d="m15 18-6-6 6-6"/>
                </svg>
            </button>

            <!-- Avatar (Long-Press Aktif) -->
            <div data-user-avatar 
                 data-user-id="{{ other_user.id }}" 
                 data-user-name="{{ other_user.ad_soyad }}" 
                 data-user-office="{{ other_user.sirket_unvani or 'Doğrulanmış Broker' }}" 
                 data-user-photo="{{ other_user.profil_foto or '' }}" 
                 class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl theme-bg-light theme-text-dark font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 overflow-hidden border theme-border-light shadow-2xs cursor-pointer select-none">
                {% if other_user.profil_foto %}
                    <img src="{{ other_user.profil_foto }}" alt="{{ other_user.ad_soyad }}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">
                {% else %}
                    {{ (other_user.ad_soyad[:2] if other_user.ad_soyad else 'EM').upper() }}
                {% endif %}
            </div>

            <div class="min-w-0">
                <h1 class="text-xs font-black text-slate-900 truncate leading-tight">{{ other_user.ad_soyad or 'Meslektaşınız' }}</h1>
                <p class="text-[10px] theme-text-primary font-semibold truncate">{{ other_user.sirket_unvani or 'Doğrulanmış Broker' }}</p>
            </div>
        </div>

        {% if other_user.telefon and not is_blocked %}
        <a href="tel:{{ other_user.telefon }}" 
           class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl theme-bg-light theme-text-dark hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95 transition border theme-border-light text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
            <span>📞</span> <span>Ara</span>
        </a>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    <!-- Mesaj Listesi -->
    <div id="messagesContainer" class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 space-y-2.5 custom-scrollbar">
        {% if messages and messages|length > 0 %}
            {% for msg in messages %}
                {% if msg.gonderen_id|string == current_user_id|string %}
                    <div class="flex justify-end message-row" data-id="{{ msg.id }}">
                        <div class="max-w-[80%] theme-bg-primary text-white rounded-2xl rounded-tr-xs px-3.5 py-2 text-xs shadow-2xs space-y-0.5">
                            <p class="leading-relaxed break-words whitespace-pre-wrap">{{ msg.mesaj_metni }}</p>
                            <div class="text-[9px] text-white/80 text-right time-label" data-utc="{{ msg.created_at }}"></div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                {% else %}
                    <div class="flex justify-start message-row" data-id="{{ msg.id }}">
                        <div class="max-w-[80%] bg-white text-slate-800 border border-slate-200/80 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-xs px-3.5 py-2 text-xs shadow-2xs space-y-0.5">
                            <p class="leading-relaxed break-words whitespace-pre-wrap">{{ msg.mesaj_metni }}</p>
                            <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 text-right time-label" data-utc="{{ msg.created_at }}"></div>
                        </div>

        {% else %}
            <div id="emptyPlaceholder" class="text-center py-16 px-4 space-y-2">
                <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full theme-bg-light theme-text-dark flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-xl border theme-border-light">
                    💬
                </div>
                <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Sohbet Başlatılmadı</p>
                <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 max-w-xs mx-auto">
                    Müşteri randevusu veya işbirliği şartları hakkında ilk mesajı yazın.
                </p>
            </div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    <!-- Mesaj Alanı -->
    {% if is_blocked %}
        <div class="p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border-t border-rose-200 text-center text-rose-700 text-xs font-bold shrink-0 space-y-1">
            <p>🚫 Bu kullanıcıyla aranızdaki iletişim engellenmiştir.</p>
            <p class="text-[10px] font-medium text-rose-600">Karşılıklı mesaj gönderimi ve işbirliği teklifleri kapatılmıştır.</p>
        </div>
    {% else %}
        <form id="chatForm" onsubmit="handleSendMessage(event)" class="p-2.5 bg-white border-t border-slate-100 shrink-0 shadow-xs">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input type="text" 
                       id="messageInput" 
                       required 
                       autocomplete="off" 
                       placeholder="Mesajınızı yazın..." 
                       class="flex-1 px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:theme-border-primary focus:outline-none transition shadow-2xs">
                
                <button type="submit" 
                        id="sendBtn"
                        class="theme-bg-primary theme-hover-bg active:scale-95 text-white p-2.5 rounded-2xl transition shadow-xs shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4 fill-current rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path d="M2.01 21L23 12 2.01 3 2 10l15 2-15 2z"/>
                    </svg>
                </button>
            </div>
        </form>
    {% endif %}
</div>

<script>
    const chatRoot = document.getElementById('chatRoot');
    const currentUserId = chatRoot.getAttribute('data-current-user-id');
    const otherUserId = chatRoot.getAttribute('data-other-user-id');
    const collabId = chatRoot.getAttribute('data-collab-id');
    const isBlocked = chatRoot.getAttribute('data-is-blocked') === 'true';

    const container = document.getElementById('messagesContainer');
    const input = document.getElementById('messageInput');

    function formatTime(isoString) {
        if (!isoString) return new Date().toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR', {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'});
        try {
            let dateStr = isoString;
            if (!dateStr.endsWith('Z') && !dateStr.includes('+')) {
                dateStr += 'Z';
            }
            const d = new Date(dateStr);
            return d.toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR', {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'});
        } catch(e) {
            return '';
        }
    }

    function localizeExistingTimes() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.time-label').forEach(el => {
            const raw = el.getAttribute('data-utc');
            if (raw) el.textContent = formatTime(raw);
        });
    }

    function scrollToBottom() {
        if (container) {
            container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        localizeExistingTimes();
        scrollToBottom();
        if (typeof attachLongPressListeners === 'function') attachLongPressListeners();
    });

    async function handleSendMessage(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (isBlocked) return;

        const text = input.value.trim();
        if (!text) return;

        input.value = '';
        input.focus();

        const ph = document.getElementById('emptyPlaceholder');
        if (ph) ph.style.display = 'none';

        const tempId = 'temp-' + Date.now();
        const localTimeStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR', {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'});

        const tempRow = document.createElement('div');
        tempRow.dataset.id = tempId;
        tempRow.className = "flex justify-end message-row";
        tempRow.innerHTML = `
            <div class="max-w-[80%] theme-bg-primary text-white rounded-2xl rounded-tr-xs px-3.5 py-2 text-xs shadow-2xs space-y-0.5">
                <p class="leading-relaxed break-words whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(text)}</p>
                <div class="text-[9px] text-white/80 text-right">${localTimeStr}</div>
            </div>
        `;
        container.appendChild(tempRow);
        scrollToBottom();

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('receiver_id', otherUserId);
        formData.append('message', text);
        if (collabId && collabId !== 'None') {
            formData.append('collab_id', collabId);
        }

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/messages/api/send', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success && data.message && data.message.id) {
                tempRow.dataset.id = data.message.id;
            } else if (!data.success && data.error) {
                alert(data.error);
                tempRow.remove();
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.error("Mesaj gönderilemedi:", err);
        }
    }

    async function pollNewMessages() {
        if (isBlocked) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/messages/api/poll/${otherUserId}`);
            if (!res.ok) return;
            const data = await res.json();
            const msgs = data.messages || [];

            if (msgs.length > 0) {
                const ph = document.getElementById('emptyPlaceholder');
                if (ph) ph.style.display = 'none';

                const existingIds = new Set(Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.message-row')).map(el => el.dataset.id).filter(Boolean));

                let hasNew = false;
                msgs.forEach(m => {
                    if (m.id && !existingIds.has(String(m.id))) {
                        hasNew = true;
                        const isMe = String(m.gonderen_id) === String(currentUserId);
                        const time = formatTime(m.created_at);
                        
                        const row = document.createElement('div');
                        row.dataset.id = m.id;
                        row.className = `flex ${isMe ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'} message-row`;
                        row.innerHTML = `
                            <div class="max-w-[80%] ${isMe ? 'theme-bg-primary text-white rounded-tr-xs' : 'bg-white text-slate-800 border border-slate-200/80 rounded-tl-xs'} rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs shadow-2xs space-y-0.5">
                                <p class="leading-relaxed break-words whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(m.mesaj_metni)}</p>
                                <div class="text-[9px] ${isMe ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-slate-400'} text-right">${time}</div>
                            </div>
                        `;
                        container.appendChild(row);
                    }
                });

                if (hasNew) scrollToBottom();
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("Mesaj yoklama hatası:", e);
        }
    }

    function escapeHtml(text) {
        return text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(m) {
            return {'&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#039;'}[m];
        });
    }

    setInterval(pollNewMessages, 2500);
</script>
{% endblock %}

            <span>Alıcı</span>

        <a href="/network/groups/{{ group.id }}?filter_type=referral" class="py-2 rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 {% if filter_type == 'referral' %}bg-white text-emerald-800 shadow-xs border border-emerald-100{% else %}text-slate-600 hover:text-emerald-700{% endif %}">
            <svg class="w-3 h-3 stroke-current shrink-0" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2.3"><path d="M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H6a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/></svg>

<script>
    function openShareModal() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('sharePostModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('sharePostContent');
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeShareModal() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('sharePostModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('sharePostContent');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function setPostType(type, btn) {
        document.getElementById('hiddenPostType').value = type;
        document.querySelectorAll('.post-type-btn').forEach(b => {
            b.className = 'post-type-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 font-bold text-[11px] transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-2xs';
        });
        btn.className = 'post-type-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900 font-extrabold text-[11px] transition shadow-xs flex flex-col items-center gap-1';

        const portSec = document.getElementById('portfolioSelectSection');
        const titleInput = document.getElementById('postTitleInput');

        if (type === 'portfolio') {
            portSec.classList.remove('hidden');
            titleInput.placeholder = "Örn: Beykoz Acarkent 5+1 Satılık Müstakil Villa";
        } else if (type === 'buyer_request') {
            portSec.classList.add('hidden');
            titleInput.placeholder = "Örn: Beşiktaş'ta 25M TL bütçeli 3+1 hazır alıcı";
        } else {
            portSec.classList.add('hidden');
            titleInput.placeholder = "Örn: Antalya Konyaaltı'nda satılık mülkü olan müşteri yönlendirilecektir";
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    async function handleReadAllNotifications() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('btnReadAll');
        if (btn) {
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.innerText = 'Okunuyor...';
        }

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/notifications/api/read-all', { method: 'POST' });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) {
                // Kartlardaki yeşil halkaları ve noktaları temizle
                document.querySelectorAll('.unread-dot').forEach(dot => dot.remove());
                document.querySelectorAll('.notif-item').forEach(item => {
                    item.classList.remove('border-emerald-300', 'ring-2', 'ring-emerald-500/10');
                    item.classList.add('border-slate-200/90');
                });
                if (btn) {
                    btn.innerText = '✓ Tümü Okundu';
                    btn.className = "px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 border border-slate-200 text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0";
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("Tümünü okundu yapma hatası:", e);
            if (btn) {
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.innerText = '✓ Tümünü Oku';
            }
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<div id="locationPickerDrawer" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-end justify-center opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-200">
    <div id="locationDrawerContent" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" class="w-full max-w-md bg-[#FAFDFB] rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-3.5 shadow-2xl border-t theme-border-primary transform translate-y-full transition-transform duration-300 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div class="w-12 h-1 bg-slate-300 rounded-full mx-auto"></div>

        <div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100">
                <h3 id="locationDrawerTitle" class="text-xs font-black theme-text-dark uppercase tracking-tight">İl Seçiniz</h3>
                <button type="button" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-lg font-bold">✕</button>
            </div>
            
            <div class="mt-2.5">
                <input type="text" id="drawerSearchInput" oninput="filterDrawerOptions()" placeholder="Listede ara..." class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:theme-border-primary shadow-2xs">
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="drawerOptionsList" class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 pr-1 py-1 custom-scrollbar"></div>

        <button type="button" onclick="closeLocationDrawer()" class="w-full py-3 bg-slate-200/80 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-2xl text-xs transition mt-2">
            Vazgeç
        </button>
    </div>
</div>

<script id="turkeyDataJson" type="application/json">
{{ turkey_data|tojson if turkey_data else '{}' }}
</script>

<script>
    const rawDataEl = document.getElementById('turkeyDataJson');
    let turkeyData = {};
    try {
        turkeyData = JSON.parse(rawDataEl.textContent || '{}');
    } catch(e) {
        turkeyData = { "İstanbul": ["Beşiktaş", "Beykoz", "Kadıköy", "Sarıyer"] };
    }

    let currentPickerMode = 'city';
    let currentPool = [];

    function openLocationDrawer(mode) {
        currentPickerMode = mode;
        const titleEl = document.getElementById('locationDrawerTitle');
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput');
        searchInput.value = '';

        const collator = new Intl.Collator('tr');

        if (mode === 'city') {
            titleEl.innerText = 'İl Seçiniz';
            currentPool = Object.keys(turkeyData).sort(collator.compare);
        } else {
            const selectedCity = document.getElementById('realCityInput').value || 'İstanbul';
            titleEl.innerText = `${selectedCity} - İlçe Seçiniz`;
            const rawDistricts = turkeyData[selectedCity] || ["Merkez"];
            currentPool = [...rawDistricts].sort(collator.compare);
        }

        renderDrawerOptions(currentPool);

        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeLocationDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function renderDrawerOptions(items) {
        const container = document.getElementById('drawerOptionsList');
        container.innerHTML = '';

        const currentActive = (currentPickerMode === 'city') 
            ? document.getElementById('realCityInput').value 
            : document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value;

        if (!items || items.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">Eşleşen sonuç bulunamadı.</div>';
            return;
        }

        items.forEach(item => {
            const isSelected = (item.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr') === currentActive.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'));
            const optBtn = document.createElement('button');
            optBtn.type = 'button';
            optBtn.onclick = () => selectLocationItem(item);

            if (isSelected) {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl theme-bg-primary text-white font-black text-xs transition shadow-xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span><span class="text-sm">✓</span>`;
            } else {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-slate-800 hover:theme-text-dark border border-slate-200/80 font-bold text-xs transition shadow-2xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span>`;
            }

            container.appendChild(optBtn);
        });
    }

    function filterDrawerOptions() {
        const query = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
        const filtered = currentPool.filter(it => it.toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(query));
        renderDrawerOptions(filtered);
    }

    function selectLocationItem(val) {
        if (currentPickerMode === 'city') {
            document.getElementById('realCityInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayCityText').innerText = val;

            const availableDistricts = turkeyData[val] || ["Merkez"];
            const defaultDistrict = availableDistricts[0];
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = defaultDistrict;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = defaultDistrict;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = val;
        }
        closeLocationDrawer();
    }

    function selectType(typeName, btn) {
        document.getElementById('selectedType').value = typeName;
        document.querySelectorAll('.type-btn').forEach(b => {
            b.className = 'type-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-500 hover:theme-border-primary shadow-2xs transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ' + (b.classList.contains('col-span-2') ? 'col-span-2' : '');
        });
        btn.className = 'type-btn p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light theme-text-dark shadow-sm ring-2 ring-slate-900/5 transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ' + (btn.classList.contains('col-span-2') ? 'col-span-2' : '');
    }

    let portFeatures = ["Müstakil Bahçeli", "Kapalı Otopark", "Açık Yüzme Havuzu"];

    function togglePortFeature(feat, btn) {
        const idx = portFeatures.indexOf(feat);
        if (idx > -1) {
            portFeatures.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = 'port-chip px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-semibold border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:theme-border-primary transition';
            btn.innerText = `+ ${feat}`;
        } else {
            portFeatures.push(feat);
            btn.className = 'port-chip px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-semibold border-2 theme-border-primary theme-bg-light theme-text-dark transition';
            btn.innerText = `✓ ${feat}`;
        }
        document.getElementById('portfolioFeaturesInput').value = portFeatures.join(', ');
        document.getElementById('selectedDonatiCount').innerText = `${portFeatures.length} seçildi`;
    }

    let selectedFilesPool = [];
    const MAX_PHOTOS = 10;

    function handleFilesSelected(event) {
        const newFiles = Array.from(event.target.files);
        if (!newFiles || newFiles.length === 0) return;

        const remainingSlots = MAX_PHOTOS - selectedFilesPool.length;
        if (remainingSlots <= 0) {
            document.getElementById('limitWarning').classList.remove('hidden');
            event.target.value = '';
            return;
        }

        const filesToAdd = newFiles.slice(0, remainingSlots);
        if (newFiles.length > remainingSlots) {
            document.getElementById('limitWarning').classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
            document.getElementById('limitWarning').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        selectedFilesPool = selectedFilesPool.concat(filesToAdd);
        event.target.value = '';

        syncRealFileInput();
        renderPhotoPreviews();
    }

    function removePhoto(index) {
        selectedFilesPool.splice(index, 1);
        document.getElementById('limitWarning').classList.add('hidden');
        syncRealFileInput();
        renderPhotoPreviews();
    }

    function syncRealFileInput() {
        const dataTransfer = new DataTransfer();
        selectedFilesPool.forEach(file => dataTransfer.items.add(file));
        document.getElementById('realFileInput').files = dataTransfer.files;

        const countBadge = document.getElementById('photoCountBadge');
        countBadge.innerText = `${selectedFilesPool.length} / ${MAX_PHOTOS} seçildi`;

        const btnAdd = document.getElementById('btnAddPhoto');
        if (selectedFilesPool.length >= MAX_PHOTOS) {
            btnAdd.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            btnAdd.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
    }

    function renderPhotoPreviews() {
        const previewContainer = document.getElementById('previewContainer');
        previewContainer.querySelectorAll('.img-preview-box').forEach(el => el.remove());

        selectedFilesPool.forEach((file, index) => {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(e) {
                const imgWrapper = document.createElement('div');
                imgWrapper.className = 'img-preview-box w-16 h-16 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border-2 theme-border-primary shadow-2xs relative group';
                imgWrapper.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="${e.target.result}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">
                    ${index === 0 ? '<span class="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 theme-bg-primary text-[8px] text-white text-center font-bold py-0.5 pointer-events-none">Kapak</span>' : ''}
                    <button type="button" onclick="removePhoto(${index})" class="absolute top-1 right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-slate-900/70 hover:bg-red-600 text-white rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] font-bold transition shadow-xs">
                        ✕
                    </button>
                `;
                previewContainer.insertBefore(imgWrapper, document.getElementById('btnAddPhoto'));
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        });
    }

    let currentStep = 1;

    function goToStep(step) {
        if (step === currentStep) return;
        setStepView(step);
    }

    function prevStep() {
        if (currentStep > 1) {
            goToStep(currentStep - 1);
        } else {
            window.location.href = '/dashboard';
        }
    }

    function nextStep() {
        if (currentStep === 1) {
            goToStep(2);
        } else if (currentStep === 2) {
            goToStep(3);
        } else if (currentStep === 3) {
            document.getElementById('portfolioForm').submit();
        }
    }

    function setStepView(step) {
        document.getElementById('step-1').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('step-2').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('step-3').classList.add('hidden');

        document.getElementById(`step-${step}`).classList.remove('hidden');

        for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
            const badge = document.getElementById(`badge-step-${i}`);
            if (i <= step) {
                badge.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full theme-bg-primary text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold transition shadow-2xs';
            } else {
                badge.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold transition';
            }
        }

        document.getElementById('line-1-2').className = step >= 2 ? 'w-8 h-0.5 theme-bg-primary transition-colors' : 'w-8 h-0.5 bg-slate-200 transition-colors';
        document.getElementById('line-2-3').className = step >= 3 ? 'w-8 h-0.5 theme-bg-primary transition-colors' : 'w-8 h-0.5 bg-slate-200 transition-colors';

        const btnNext = document.getElementById('btnNext');
        if (step === 3) {
            btnNext.innerText = 'Uygun Emlakçıları Bul';
        } else {
            btnNext.innerText = 'Devam';
        }

        currentStep = step;
    }

    function toggleYetki() {
        const cb = document.getElementById('yetkiCheckbox');
        const box = document.getElementById('yetkiBox');
        const icon = document.getElementById('yetkiCheckIcon');
        const text = document.getElementById('yetkiText');

        cb.checked = !cb.checked;

        if (cb.checked) {
            box.className = 'p-3 theme-bg-light border-2 theme-border-primary rounded-xl flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer select-none transition-all shadow-2xs';
            icon.className = 'w-5 h-5 rounded theme-bg-primary text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold transition';
            icon.innerText = '✓';
            text.className = 'text-xs font-bold theme-text-dark';
            text.innerText = 'Satış yetkim mevcut';
        } else {
            box.className = 'p-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer select-none transition-all shadow-2xs';
            icon.className = 'w-5 h-5 rounded border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 text-transparent flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold transition';
            icon.innerText = '';
            text.className = 'text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500';
            text.innerText = 'Satış yetkim bulunmuyor (Paylaşım)';
        }
    }

    function selectVisibility(visType, card) {
        document.getElementById('selectedVisibility').value = visType;

        document.querySelectorAll('.vis-card').forEach(c => {
            c.className = 'vis-card flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-50 transition shadow-2xs';
            const circle = c.querySelector('.radio-circle');
            const dot = c.querySelector('.dot');
            circle.className = 'w-4 h-4 rounded-full border border-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center radio-circle';
            dot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent dot';
        });

        card.className = 'vis-card flex items-center justify-between p-3 theme-bg-light border-2 theme-border-primary rounded-2xl cursor-pointer transition shadow-2xs';
        const circle = card.querySelector('.radio-circle');
        const dot = card.querySelector('.dot');
        circle.className = 'w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 theme-border-primary flex items-center justify-center radio-circle';
        dot.className = 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full theme-bg-primary dot';
    }

    function formatPriceInput(input) {
        let rawVal = input.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
        document.getElementById('realFiyatInput').value = rawVal;
        if (rawVal) {
            input.value = Number(rawVal).toLocaleString('tr-TR');
        } else {
            input.value = '';
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const defaultFiyat = document.getElementById('realFiyatInput').value;
        if (defaultFiyat) {
            document.getElementById('formattedFiyatInput').value = Number(defaultFiyat).toLocaleString('tr-TR');
        }
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

            </div>
            <div>
                <span class="text-slate-400 font-semibold block mb-1">Nitelikler & Açıklama</span>
                <p id="modDesc" class="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl text-slate-700 text-[11px] leading-relaxed border border-slate-100"></p>
            </div>
        </div>

        <button onclick="closeDetailModal(true)" class="w-full bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs transition">
            Kapat
        </button>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- ================= 2. TAM EKRAN LIGHTBOX ================= -->
<div id="lightboxModal" onclick="closeLightbox()" class="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-black/95 flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-200 p-2 select-none">
    <div class="absolute top-4 inset-x-4 flex items-center justify-between z-10" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
        <span id="lightboxCounter" class="text-white text-xs font-bold bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-white/10">1 / 1</span>
        <button onclick="closeLightbox()" class="text-white text-xl font-bold bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-md w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-white/30 active:scale-95 transition">✕</button>
    </div>

    <button id="btnPrevPhoto" onclick="prevLightboxPhoto(event)" class="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-10 w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/30 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-2xl font-bold backdrop-blur-md transition active:scale-90 shadow-lg border border-white/10">
        ‹
    </button>

    <div class="max-w-full max-h-[80vh] flex items-center justify-center p-2" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
        <img id="lightboxImage" src="" alt="Tam Boy Görsel" class="max-w-full max-h-[80vh] rounded-2xl object-contain shadow-2xl transition duration-200">
    </div>

    <button id="btnNextPhoto" onclick="nextLightboxPhoto(event)" class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-10 w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/30 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-2xl font-bold backdrop-blur-md transition active:scale-90 shadow-lg border border-white/10">
        ›
    </button>
</div>

<!-- ================= 3. BASILI TUTUNCA AÇILAN AKSİYON ÇEKMECESİ ================= -->
<div id="actionSheet" onclick="closeActionSheet()" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-end justify-center opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-200">
    <div id="actionContent" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" class="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-3 shadow-2xl transform translate-y-full transition-transform duration-300">
        <div class="w-12 h-1 bg-slate-200 rounded-full mx-auto mb-1"></div>
        <div class="text-center pb-2">
            <h4 class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">Portföy İşlemleri</h4>
            <p id="actionTargetTitle" class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 truncate"></p>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-2 text-xs font-bold">
            <a id="editLink" href="#" class="w-full py-3 px-4 bg-slate-50 hover:theme-bg-light theme-text-dark rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition border border-slate-200/60">
                <span class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4 stroke-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2"><path d="M12 20h9"/><path d="M16.5 3.5a2.121 2.121 0 0 1 3 3L7 19l-4 1 1-4L16.5 3.5z"/></svg>
                    <span>Portföyü Düzenle</span>
                </span>
                <span class="text-slate-400 text-sm">›</span>
            </a>

            <form id="statusForm" method="POST" action="">
                <button type="submit" class="w-full py-3 px-4 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition border border-slate-200/60">
                    <span class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span id="statusIcon">
                            <svg class="w-4 h-4 stroke-current text-slate-600" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2"><rect x="6" y="4" width="4" height="16"></rect><rect x="14" y="4" width="4" height="16"></rect></svg>
                        </span>
                        <span id="statusBtnText">Durumu Değiştir</span>
                    </span>
                    <span class="text-slate-400 text-sm">›</span>
                </button>
            </form>

            <form id="deleteForm" method="POST" action="" onsubmit="return confirm('Bu portföyü kalıcı olarak silmek istediğinizden emin misiniz?');">
                <button type="submit" class="w-full py-3 px-4 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition border border-red-200/70">
                    <span class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <svg class="w-4 h-4 stroke-current text-red-600" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2"><polyline points="3 6 5 6 21 6"></polyline><path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6m3 0V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"></path></svg>
                        <span>Portföyü Sil</span>
                    </span>
                    <span class="text-red-400 text-sm">›</span>
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>

        <button type="button" onclick="closeActionSheet()" class="w-full bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-xs transition mt-2">
            Vazgeç
        </button>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    let pressTimer = null;
    let isLongPress = false;
    let touchStartX = 0;
    let touchStartY = 0;
    let isScrolling = false;
    let lastModalOpenTime = 0;

    let currentGalleryImages = [];
    let currentLightboxIndex = 0;

    document.querySelectorAll('.port-card').forEach(card => {
        card.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
            if (e.target.closest('a') || e.target.closest('button')) return;

            isLongPress = false;
            isScrolling = false;
            touchStartX = e.touches[0].clientX;
            touchStartY = e.touches[0].clientY;

            pressTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                isLongPress = true;
                card.classList.add('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
                if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(50);
                openActionSheet(card);
            }, 500);
        }, { passive: true });

        card.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
            if (e.target.closest('a') || e.target.closest('button')) return;

            const deltaX = Math.abs(e.touches[0].clientX - touchStartX);
            const deltaY = Math.abs(e.touches[0].clientY - touchStartY);

            if (deltaX > 10 || deltaY > 10) {
                isScrolling = true;
                clearTimeout(pressTimer);
                card.classList.remove('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
            }
        }, { passive: true });

        card.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
            if (e.target.closest('a') || e.target.closest('button')) {
                clearTimeout(pressTimer);
                card.classList.remove('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
                return;
            }

            clearTimeout(pressTimer);
            card.classList.remove('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');

            if (isLongPress || isScrolling) return;

            if (e.cancelable) {
                e.preventDefault();
            }
            openDetailModal(card);
        });

        card.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
            if (e.target.closest('a') || e.target.closest('button')) return;
            if (e.button !== 0) return;
            isLongPress = false;
            pressTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                isLongPress = true;
                card.classList.add('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
                openActionSheet(card);
            }, 500);
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
            if (e.target.closest('a') || e.target.closest('button')) return;
            if (e.button !== 0) return;
            clearTimeout(pressTimer);
            card.classList.remove('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
            if (!isLongPress) {
                openDetailModal(card);
            }
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            clearTimeout(pressTimer);
            card.classList.remove('scale-95', 'ring-2', 'theme-border-primary');
        });
    });

    function openDetailModal(card) {
        lastModalOpenTime = Date.now();

        document.getElementById('modType').innerText = card.dataset.type;
        document.getElementById('modTitle').innerText = card.dataset.title;
        document.getElementById('modPrice').innerText = card.dataset.price;
        document.getElementById('modLocation').innerText = card.dataset.location;
        document.getElementById('modOda').innerText = card.dataset.oda;
        document.getElementById('modM2').innerText = card.dataset.m2;
        document.getElementById('modYetki').innerText = card.dataset.yetki;
        document.getElementById('modDesc').innerText = card.dataset.desc;

        const galleryContainer = document.getElementById('modalGalleryContainer');
        galleryContainer.innerHTML = '';

        try {
            currentGalleryImages = JSON.parse(card.dataset.images || '[]');
        } catch(err) {
            currentGalleryImages = [];
        }

        if (!currentGalleryImages || currentGalleryImages.length === 0) {
            currentGalleryImages = ["https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1600585154340-be6161a56a0c?auto=format&fit=crop&w=600&q=80"];
        }

        document.getElementById('galleryCountBadge').innerText = `${currentGalleryImages.length} Fotoğraf`;

        currentGalleryImages.forEach((imgUrl, idx) => {
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.className = 'w-48 h-32 shrink-0 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 relative snap-start cursor-pointer border border-slate-200 group';
            item.innerHTML = `
                <img src="${imgUrl}" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-300">
                <span class="absolute bottom-1.5 left-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs text-white text-[9px] font-bold rounded-md">
                    ${idx + 1} / ${currentGalleryImages.length}
                </span>
            `;
            item.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openLightbox(idx);
            };
            galleryContainer.appendChild(item);
        });

        const modal = document.getElementById('detailModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('detailContent');
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeDetailModal(force = false) {
        if (!force && Date.now() - lastModalOpenTime < 400) {
            return;
        }
        const modal = document.getElementById('detailModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('detailContent');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function openLightbox(index) {
        if (!currentGalleryImages || currentGalleryImages.length === 0) return;
        currentLightboxIndex = index;
        updateLightboxView();

        const lb = document.getElementById('lightboxModal');
        lb.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');

        const nav = document.getElementById('bottomNavContainer');
        if (nav && nav.parentElement) {
            nav.parentElement.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }

    function updateLightboxView() {
        const total = currentGalleryImages.length;
        const currentUrl = currentGalleryImages[currentLightboxIndex];
        
        document.getElementById('lightboxImage').src = currentUrl;
        document.getElementById('lightboxCounter').innerText = `${currentLightboxIndex + 1} / ${total}`;

        const btnPrev = document.getElementById('btnPrevPhoto');
        const btnNext = document.getElementById('btnNextPhoto');
        if (total <= 1) {
            btnPrev.classList.add('hidden');
            btnNext.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            btnPrev.classList.remove('hidden');
            btnNext.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
    }

    function prevLightboxPhoto(e) {
        if (e) e.stopPropagation();
        if (currentLightboxIndex > 0) {
            currentLightboxIndex--;
        } else {
            currentLightboxIndex = currentGalleryImages.length - 1;
        }
        updateLightboxView();
    }

    function nextLightboxPhoto(e) {
        if (e) e.stopPropagation();
        if (currentLightboxIndex < currentGalleryImages.length - 1) {
            currentLightboxIndex++;
        } else {
            currentLightboxIndex = 0;
        }
        updateLightboxView();
    }

    function closeLightbox() {
        const lb = document.getElementById('lightboxModal');
        lb.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');

        const nav = document.getElementById('bottomNavContainer');
        if (nav && nav.parentElement) {
            nav.parentElement.style.display = '';
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        const lb = document.getElementById('lightboxModal');
        if (!lb.classList.contains('pointer-events-none')) {
            if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') prevLightboxPhoto();
            if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') nextLightboxPhoto();
            if (e.key === 'Escape') closeLightbox();
        }
    });

    function openActionSheet(card) {
        const id = card.dataset.id;
        const currentStatus = (card.dataset.status || '').trim().toLowerCase();

        document.getElementById('actionTargetTitle').innerText = card.dataset.title;
        document.getElementById('editLink').href = `/portfolios/edit/${id}`;
        document.getElementById('deleteForm').action = `/portfolios/delete/${id}`;
        document.getElementById('statusForm').action = `/portfolios/toggle-status/${id}`;

        const statusForm = document.getElementById('statusForm');
        const editLink = document.getElementById('editLink');

        if (currentStatus === 'satıldı') {
            statusForm.classList.add('hidden');
            editLink.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            statusForm.classList.remove('hidden');
            editLink.classList.remove('hidden');

            const statusBtnText = document.getElementById('statusBtnText');
            if (currentStatus === 'aktif') {
                statusBtnText.innerText = 'Yayından Kaldır (Pasife Al)';
            } else {
                statusBtnText.innerText = 'Tekrar Yayına Al (Aktif Et)';
            }
        }

        const sheet = document.getElementById('actionSheet');
        const content = document.getElementById('actionContent');
        sheet.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeActionSheet() {
        const sheet = document.getElementById('actionSheet');
        const content = document.getElementById('actionContent');
        sheet.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block title %}Profili Tamamla / Düzenle - EMLAKMATCH{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<div class="max-w-md mx-auto space-y-4 pb-16">
    <!-- Üst Bar -->
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 pb-1 border-b border-slate-200/80">
        <button type="button" onclick="if(document.referrer){history.back();}else{window.location.href='/profile';}" class="p-1 -ml-1 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 transition">
            <svg class="w-6 h-6 stroke-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="m15 18-6-6 6-6"/></svg>
        </button>
        <h1 class="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Profili Tamamla / Düzenle</h1>
        <div class="w-6"></div>
    </div>

    {% if first_login %}
    <div class="p-3.5 theme-bg-light border theme-border-light rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold theme-text-dark shadow-2xs">
        👋 Aramıza hoş geldiniz! Portföy paylaşabilmek ve ağa tam erişim sağlayabilmek için lütfen bilgilerinizi eksiksiz tamamlayınız.
    </div>
    {% endif %}

    <form action="/profile/update" method="POST" enctype="multipart/form-data" class="bg-white p-5 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-2xs space-y-4">
        <div>
            <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Ad Soyad</label>
            <input type="text" name="ad_soyad" required value="{{ user.ad_soyad or '' }}" placeholder="Adınız Soyadınız"

<script>
    function updateProfileFileText(input, labelId) {
        const lbl = document.getElementById(labelId);
        if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
            lbl.innerText = input.files[0].name;
            lbl.className = "text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate";
        } else {
            lbl.innerText = "Dosya seçilmedi";
            lbl.className = "text-xs text-slate-500 truncate font-medium";
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    let currentDrawerImages = [];
    let currentDrawerPhotoIndex = 0;

    function handlePortfolioClick(card) {
        const root = document.getElementById('profileRoot');
        const isOwner = root && root.getAttribute('data-is-me') === '1';

        const portId = card.getAttribute('data-id');
        const portType = card.getAttribute('data-tip') || 'MÜLK';
        const portIlce = card.getAttribute('data-ilce') || '';
        const portMahalle = card.getAttribute('data-mahalle') || '';
        const portFiyat = card.getAttribute('data-fiyat');
        const portOda = card.getAttribute('data-oda') || '-';
        const portM2 = card.getAttribute('data-m2') || '-';
        const portDurum = card.getAttribute('data-durum') || 'Aktif';
        const portDesc = card.getAttribute('data-aciklama') || '';
        const portAnaFoto = card.getAttribute('data-anafoto');

        try {
            currentDrawerImages = JSON.parse(card.getAttribute('data-fotolar') || '[]');
        } catch(e) {
            currentDrawerImages = [];
        }

        if (!currentDrawerImages || currentDrawerImages.length === 0) {
            currentDrawerImages = portAnaFoto ? [portAnaFoto] : ['https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1600585154340-be6161a56a0c?auto=format&fit=crop&w=600&q=80'];
        }

        currentDrawerPhotoIndex = 0;
        updateDrawerPhotoView();

        document.getElementById('drawerPortType').innerText = portType;
        document.getElementById('drawerPortTitle').innerText = portIlce + (portMahalle ? ' - ' + portMahalle : '');
        document.getElementById('drawerPortStatus').innerText = portDurum;

        let priceText = 'Fiyat Belirtilmedi';
        if (portFiyat && !isNaN(portFiyat) && Number(portFiyat) > 0) {
            priceText = new Intl.NumberFormat('tr-TR').format(Number(portFiyat)) + ' TL';
        }
        document.getElementById('drawerPortPrice').innerText = priceText;
        document.getElementById('drawerPortLocation').innerText = 'İstanbul / ' + portIlce;
        document.getElementById('drawerPortOda').innerText = portOda;
        document.getElementById('drawerPortM2').innerText = portM2.includes('m²') ? portM2 : (portM2 + ' m²');

        const featContainer = document.getElementById('drawerPortFeaturesList');
        featContainer.innerHTML = '';
        const featuresArray = portDesc ? portDesc.split(',').map(f => f.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];

        if (featuresArray.length > 0) {
            featuresArray.forEach(f => {
                const chip = document.createElement('span');
                chip.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 theme-bg-light theme-text-dark border theme-border-light text-[11px] font-bold rounded-xl shadow-2xs';
                chip.innerText = '✓ ' + f;
                featContainer.appendChild(chip);
            });
        } else {
            featContainer.innerHTML = '<span class="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">Belirtilmiş bir nitelik bulunmuyor.</span>';
        }

        const actionContainer = document.getElementById('drawerPortActions');
        if (isOwner) {
            actionContainer.innerHTML = `
                <a href="/portfolios/edit/${portId}" class="w-full py-3 theme-bg-primary theme-hover-bg text-white font-bold rounded-2xl text-xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-xs">
                    <span>Portföyü Düzenle</span>
                </a>
            `;
        } else {
            actionContainer.innerHTML = `
                <a href="/collaborations/send?ilan_id=${portId}" class="w-full py-3 theme-bg-primary theme-hover-bg text-white font-bold rounded-2xl text-xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-xs">
                    <span>İşbirliği Teklifi Gönder</span>
                </a>
            `;
        }

        const modal = document.getElementById('portfolioDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('portfolioDrawerContent');
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function updateDrawerPhotoView() {
        const total = currentDrawerImages.length;
        const img = document.getElementById('drawerPortImg');
        const badge = document.getElementById('drawerPhotoBadge');
        const btnPrev = document.getElementById('btnDrawerPrev');
        const btnNext = document.getElementById('btnDrawerNext');

        img.src = currentDrawerImages[currentDrawerPhotoIndex];
        badge.innerText = `${currentDrawerPhotoIndex + 1} / ${total}`;

        if (total <= 1) {
            btnPrev.classList.add('hidden');
            btnNext.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            btnPrev.classList.remove('hidden');
            btnNext.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
    }

    function prevDrawerPhoto(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (currentDrawerPhotoIndex > 0) {
            currentDrawerPhotoIndex--;
        } else {
            currentDrawerPhotoIndex = currentDrawerImages.length - 1;
        }
        updateDrawerPhotoView();
    }

    function nextDrawerPhoto(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (currentDrawerPhotoIndex < currentDrawerImages.length - 1) {
            currentDrawerPhotoIndex++;
        } else {
            currentDrawerPhotoIndex = 0;
        }
        updateDrawerPhotoView();
    }

    function closePortfolioDrawer() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('portfolioDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('portfolioDrawerContent');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function openReviewsModal() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('reviewsModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('reviewsContent');
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeReviewsModal() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('reviewsModal');
        const content = document.getElementById('reviewsContent');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    // 16 Renkli Emlakçı Tema JS Motoru
    const agentColorThemes = [
        { name: "Zümrüt", primary: "#059669", dark: "#047857", light: "#ecfdf5", border: "#a7f3d0" },
        { name: "Safir Mavi", primary: "#2563eb", dark: "#1d4ed8", light: "#eff6ff", border: "#bfdbfe" },
        { name: "Lüks Altın", primary: "#d97706", dark: "#b45309", light: "#fffbeb", border: "#fde68a" },
        { name: "Gece Moru", primary: "#7c3aed", dark: "#6d28d9", light: "#f5f3ff", border: "#ddd6fe" },
        { name: "Okyanus Turkuaz", primary: "#0891b2", dark: "#0e7490", light: "#ecfeff", border: "#a5f3fc" },
        { name: "Kraliyet Bordo", primary: "#e11d48", dark: "#be123c", light: "#fff1f2", border: "#fecdd3" },
        { name: "Derin Çivit", primary: "#4f46e5", dark: "#4338ca", light: "#eef2ff", border: "#c7d2fe" },
        { name: "Bakır Bronz", primary: "#ea580c", dark: "#c2410c", light: "#fff7ed", border: "#fed7aa" },
        { name: "Orman Yeşili", primary: "#16a34a", dark: "#15803d", light: "#f0fdf4", border: "#bbf7d0" },
        { name: "Füme Grafit", primary: "#475569", dark: "#334155", light: "#f8fafc", border: "#cbd5e1" },
        { name: "Gök Mavisi", primary: "#0284c7", dark: "#0369a1", light: "#f0f9ff", border: "#bae6fd" },
        { name: "Gül Kurusu", primary: "#db2777", dark: "#be185d", light: "#fdf2f8", border: "#fbcfe8" },
        { name: "Zeytin Haki", primary: "#65a30d", dark: "#4d7c0f", light: "#f7fee7", border: "#d9f99d" },
        { name: "Asil Amber", primary: "#f59e0b", dark: "#d97706", light: "#fffbeb", border: "#fde68a" },
        { name: "Mercan", primary: "#f43f5e", dark: "#e11d48", light: "#fff1f2", border: "#fecdd3" },
        { name: "Koyu Petrol", primary: "#0f766e", dark: "#115e59", light: "#f0fdfa", border: "#99f6e4" }
    ];

    function applyAgentTheme(theme) {
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-primary', theme.primary);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-dark', theme.dark);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-light', theme.light);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--brand-border', theme.border);

        localStorage.setItem('agent_theme_color', theme.primary);
        localStorage.setItem('agent_theme_dark', theme.dark);
        localStorage.setItem('agent_theme_light', theme.light);
        localStorage.setItem('agent_theme_border', theme.border);
        localStorage.setItem('agent_theme_name', theme.name);

        const badge = document.getElementById('agentThemeBadge');
        if (badge) badge.innerText = theme.name;
        renderAgentPalette();
    }

    function resetAgentDefaultTheme() {
        applyAgentTheme(agentColorThemes[0]);
    }

    function renderAgentPalette() {
        const container = document.getElementById('agentPaletteContainer');
        if (!container) return;

        const currentPrimary = localStorage.getItem('agent_theme_color') || "#059669";
        const currentName = localStorage.getItem('agent_theme_name') || "Zümrüt";
        const badge = document.getElementById('agentThemeBadge');
        if (badge) badge.innerText = currentName;

        container.innerHTML = '';

        agentColorThemes.forEach(t => {
            const isSelected = (t.primary.toLowerCase() === currentPrimary.toLowerCase());
            const btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.title = t.name;
            btn.onclick = () => applyAgentTheme(t);
            btn.className = `h-8 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-150 transform hover:scale-105 ${isSelected ? 'ring-2 ring-slate-900 ring-offset-2 scale-105 shadow-sm' : 'border border-black/10'}`;
            btn.style.backgroundColor = t.primary;

            if (isSelected) {
                btn.innerHTML = `<span class="text-white text-xs font-black drop-shadow-xs">✓</span>`;
            }

            container.appendChild(btn);
        });
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        renderAgentPalette();
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

    <!-- Üst Başlık & Tescillerim Butonu -->
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">

{% block content %}
<div class="max-w-md mx-auto min-h-[85vh] flex flex-col justify-between pb-6">
    <div>
        <!-- Üst Bar & Geri Tuşu -->

        </div>

        <form id="buyerSearchForm" action="/requests/buyer/add" method="POST" class="space-y-4 mt-3">
            <!-- 1. Lokasyon -->
            <div>
                <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1.5">Müşterinin Aradığı Bölge</label>
                <div class="space-y-2">
                    <select name="il" id="ilSelect" onchange="onCityChange()" required class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none shadow-2xs">
                        <option value="">İl Seçiniz</option>
                    </select>

                    <select name="ilce" id="ilceSelect" required class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none shadow-2xs">
                        <option value="">İlçe Seçiniz</option>
                    </select>

                    <input type="text" name="mahalle" placeholder="Öncelikli Mahalle / Site (Opsiyonel)" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none shadow-2xs">
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 2. Gayrimenkul Tipi -->
            <div>
                <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1.5">Gayrimenkul Tipi</label>
                <div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 text-center text-xs">
                    <label class="p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white font-bold text-slate-700 cursor-pointer has-checked:border-2 has-checked:border-emerald-600 has-checked:bg-emerald-50/80 has-checked:text-emerald-900 transition">
                        <input type="radio" name="gayrimenkul_tipi" value="Daire" class="hidden">
                        <span>Daire</span>
                    </label>
                    <label class="p-2.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50/80 font-bold text-emerald-900 cursor-pointer has-checked:border-2 has-checked:border-emerald-600 has-checked:bg-emerald-50/80 has-checked:text-emerald-900 transition">
                        <input type="radio" name="gayrimenkul_tipi" value="Villa" checked class="hidden">
                        <span>Villa</span>
                    </label>
                    <label class="p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white font-bold text-slate-700 cursor-pointer has-checked:border-2 has-checked:border-emerald-600 has-checked:bg-emerald-50/80 has-checked:text-emerald-900 transition">
                        <input type="radio" name="gayrimenkul_tipi" value="Arsa" class="hidden">
                        <span>Arsa</span>
                    </label>
                    <label class="p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white font-bold text-slate-700 cursor-pointer has-checked:border-2 has-checked:border-emerald-600 has-checked:bg-emerald-50/80 has-checked:text-emerald-900 transition">
                        <input type="radio" name="gayrimenkul_tipi" value="İş Yeri" class="hidden">
                        <span>İş Yeri</span>
                    </label>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 3. Bütçe ve Minimum Oda -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Maksimum Bütçe (TL)</label>
                    <input type="number" name="fiyat_max" required value="45000000" placeholder="45000000" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none shadow-2xs">
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Minimum Oda Sayısı</label>
                    <input type="text" name="oda" value="4+1" placeholder="Örn: 3+1" class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none shadow-2xs">
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 4. 40 Donatı Seçimi -->
            <div>
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">Müşterinin Aradığı Donatılar</label>
                    <span id="selectedCountBadge" class="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-emerald-100">0 seçildi</span>
                </div>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mb-2">Her eşleşen özellik %40'lık donatı puanına eşit olarak katkı sağlar.</p>

                <input type="hidden" name="secilen_ozellikler" id="selectedFeaturesInput" value="">

                <div class="max-h-52 overflow-y-auto p-2 bg-slate-50/80 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 text-xs">
                    {% for feat in all_features %}
                    <button type="button" 
                            onclick="toggleFilterFeature('{{ feat }}', this)"
                            class="filter-chip px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-semibold border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:border-emerald-300 transition shadow-2xs">
                        + {{ feat }}

    <!-- Tara & Eşleştir Butonu -->
    <div class="pt-4">
        <button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('buyerSearchForm').submit()" class="w-full bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-[0.99] text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm transition shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <span>🎯</span>
            <span>Portföyleri Tara & Eşleştir</span>
        </button>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    const allProvinces = [
        "Adana", "Adıyaman", "Afyonkarahisar", "Ağrı", "Amasya", "Ankara", "Antalya", "Artvin", "Aydın", "Balıkesir",
        "Bilecik", "Bingöl", "Bitlis", "Bolu", "Burdur", "Bursa", "Çanakkale", "Çankırı", "Çorum", "Denizli",
        "Diyarbakır", "Edirne", "Elazığ", "Erzincan", "Erzurum", "Eskişehir", "Gaziantep", "Giresun", "Gümüşhane", "Hakkari",
        "Hatay", "Isparta", "Mersin", "İstanbul", "İzmir", "Kars", "Kastamonu", "Kayseri", "Kırklareli", "Kırşehir",
        "Kocaeli", "Konya", "Kütahya", "Malatya", "Manisa", "Kahramanmaraş", "Mardin", "Muğla", "Muş", "Nevşehir",
        "Niğde", "Ordu", "Rize", "Sakarya", "Samsun", "Siirt", "Sinop", "Sivas", "Tekirdağ", "Tokat",
        "Trabzon", "Tunceli", "Şanlıurfa", "Uşak", "Van", "Yozgat", "Zonguldak", "Aksaray", "Bayburt", "Karaman",
        "Kırıkkale", "Batman", "Şırnak", "Bartın", "Ardahan", "Iğdır", "Yalova", "Karabük", "Kilis", "Osmaniye", "Düzce"
    ];

    const turkeyData = {
        "İstanbul": ["Adalar", "Arnavutköy", "Ataşehir", "Avcılar", "Bağcılar", "Bahçelievler", "Bakırköy", "Başakşehir", "Bayrampaşa", "Beşiktaş", "Beykoz", "Beylikdüzü", "Beyoğlu", "Büyükçekmece", "Çatalca", "Çekmeköy", "Esenler", "Esenyurt", "Eyüpsultan", "Fatih", "Gaziosmanpaşa", "Güngören", "Kadıköy", "Kağıthane", "Kartal", "Küçükçekmece", "Maltepe", "Pendik", "Sancaktepe", "Sarıyer", "Silivri", "Sultanbeyli", "Sultangazi", "Şile", "Şişli", "Tuzla", "Ümraniye", "Üsküdar", "Zeytinburnu"],
        "Ankara": ["Akyurt", "Altındağ", "Ayaş", "Bala", "Beypazarı", "Çamlıdere", "Çankaya", "Çubuk", "Elmadağ", "Etimesgut", "Evren", "Gölbaşı", "Güdül", "Haymana", "Kahramankazan", "Kalecik", "Keçiören", "Kızılcahamam", "Mamak", "Nallıhan", "Polatlı", "Pursaklar", "Sincan", "Şereflikoçhisar", "Yenimahalle"],
        "İzmir": ["Aliağa", "Balçova", "Bayındır", "Bayraklı", "Bergama", "Beydağ", "Bornova", "Buca", "Çeşme", "Çiğli", "Dikili", "Foça", "Gaziemir", "Güzelbahçe", "Karabağlar", "Karaburun", "Karşıyaka", "Kemalpaşa", "Kınık", "Kiraz", "Konak", "Menderes", "Menemen", "Narlıdere", "Ödemiş", "Seferihisar", "Selçuk", "Tire", "Torbalı", "Urla"]
    };

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const ilSelect = document.getElementById('ilSelect');
        const collator = new Intl.Collator('tr');
        allProvinces.sort(collator.compare);

        allProvinces.forEach(il => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = il;
            opt.innerText = il;
            if (il === "İstanbul") opt.selected = true;
            ilSelect.appendChild(opt);
        });
        onCityChange();
    });

    function onCityChange() {
        const il = document.getElementById('ilSelect').value;
        const ilceSelect = document.getElementById('ilceSelect');
        ilceSelect.innerHTML = '';

        const districts = turkeyData[il] || ["Merkez", "Diğer"];
        districts.forEach(d => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = d;
            opt.innerText = d;
            if (d === "Beykoz") opt.selected = true;
            ilceSelect.appendChild(opt);
        });
    }

    let chosenFeatures = [];

    function toggleFilterFeature(feat, btn) {
        const idx = chosenFeatures.indexOf(feat);
        if (idx > -1) {
            chosenFeatures.splice(idx, 1);
            btn.className = 'filter-chip px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-semibold border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:border-emerald-300 transition shadow-2xs';
            btn.innerText = `+ ${feat}`;
        } else {
            chosenFeatures.push(feat);
            btn.className = 'filter-chip px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold border-2 border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900 transition shadow-xs';
            btn.innerText = `✓ ${feat}`;
        }
        document.getElementById('selectedFeaturesInput').value = chosenFeatures.join(',');
        document.getElementById('selectedCountBadge').innerText = `${chosenFeatures.length} seçildi`;
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block title %}Eşleşen Portföyler - EMLAKMATCH{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<div class="space-y-4 pb-8 max-w-md mx-auto">
    <!-- Üst Başlık & Geri Tuşu -->
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100">
        <a href="/requests/buyer/add" class="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 transition">
            <svg class="w-6 h-6 stroke-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5"><path d="m15 18-6-6 6-6"/></svg>
        </a>

            <div class="p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
                Seçilen bölgede aktif danışman bulunamadı.
            </div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>
    {% endif %}
</div>

<script id="turkeyDataJson" type="application/json">
{{ turkey_data|tojson if turkey_data else '{}' }}
</script>

<script>
    const rawDataEl = document.getElementById('turkeyDataJson');
    let turkeyData = {};
    try {
        turkeyData = JSON.parse(rawDataEl.textContent || '{}');
    } catch(e) {
        turkeyData = { "İstanbul": ["Beşiktaş", "Beykoz", "Kadıköy", "Sarıyer"] };
    }

    let currentPickerMode = 'city';
    let currentPool = [];

    function openLocationDrawer(mode) {
        currentPickerMode = mode;
        const titleEl = document.getElementById('locationDrawerTitle');
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput');
        searchInput.value = '';

        const collator = new Intl.Collator('tr');

        if (mode === 'city') {
            titleEl.innerText = 'İl Seçiniz';
            currentPool = Object.keys(turkeyData).sort(collator.compare);
        } else {
            const selectedCity = document.getElementById('realCityInput').value || 'İstanbul';
            titleEl.innerText = `${selectedCity} - İlçe Seçiniz`;
            const rawDistricts = turkeyData[selectedCity] || ["Merkez"];
            currentPool = [...rawDistricts].sort(collator.compare);
        }

        renderDrawerOptions(currentPool);

        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeLocationDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('locationPickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('locationDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function renderDrawerOptions(items) {
        const container = document.getElementById('drawerOptionsList');
        container.innerHTML = '';

        const currentActive = (currentPickerMode === 'city') 
            ? document.getElementById('realCityInput').value 
            : document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value;

        if (!items || items.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">Eşleşen sonuç bulunamadı.</div>';
            return;
        }

        items.forEach(item => {
            const isSelected = (item.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr') === currentActive.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'));
            const optBtn = document.createElement('button');
            optBtn.type = 'button';
            optBtn.onclick = () => selectLocationItem(item);

            if (isSelected) {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl theme-bg-primary text-white font-black text-xs transition shadow-xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span><span class="text-sm">✓</span>`;
            } else {
                optBtn.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white hover:theme-bg-light text-slate-800 hover:theme-text-dark border border-slate-200/80 font-bold text-xs transition shadow-2xs';
                optBtn.innerHTML = `<span>${item}</span>`;
            }

            container.appendChild(optBtn);
        });
    }

    function filterDrawerOptions() {
        const query = document.getElementById('drawerSearchInput').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
        const filtered = currentPool.filter(it => it.toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(query));
        renderDrawerOptions(filtered);
    }

    function selectLocationItem(val) {
        if (currentPickerMode === 'city') {
            document.getElementById('realCityInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayCityText').innerText = val;

            const availableDistricts = turkeyData[val] || ["Merkez"];
            const defaultDistrict = availableDistricts[0];
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = defaultDistrict;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = defaultDistrict;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('realDistrictInput').value = val;
            document.getElementById('displayDistrictText').innerText = val;
        }
        closeLocationDrawer();
    }

    // Gayrimenkul Tipi Çekmecesi
    function openTypeDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('typePickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('typeDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
    }

    function closeTypeDrawer() {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('typePickerDrawer');
        const content = document.getElementById('typeDrawerContent');
        drawer.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        content.classList.add('translate-y-full');
    }

    function selectPropertyType(type) {
        document.getElementById('realTypeInput').value = type;
        document.getElementById('displayTypeText').innerText = type;
        closeTypeDrawer();
    }

    // Fiyat Noktalama
    function formatPriceField(input) {
        let rawVal = input.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
        document.getElementById('realButceInput').value = rawVal || '0';
        if (rawVal) {
            input.value = Number(rawVal).toLocaleString('tr-TR');
        } else {
            input.value = '';
        }
    }

    // İki Parçalı Komisyon ve %100 Kontrolü
    function handleCommissionChange(source) {
        const senderInput = document.getElementById('senderRateInput');
        const closerInput = document.getElementById('closerRateInput');
        const totalBadge = document.getElementById('commissionTotalBadge');
        const errorMsg = document.getElementById('commissionErrorMsg');
        const submitBtn = document.getElementById('btnSubmitReferral');
        const finalHidden = document.getElementById('finalCommissionCondition');

        let sVal = parseInt(senderInput.value) || 0;
        let cVal = parseInt(closerInput.value) || 0;

        if (source === 'sender' && sVal <= 100) {
            cVal = 100 - sVal;
            closerInput.value = cVal;
        } else if (source === 'closer' && cVal <= 100) {
            sVal = 100 - cVal;
            senderInput.value = sVal;
        }

        const total = sVal + cVal;
        totalBadge.innerText = `Toplam %${total}`;

        if (total > 100 || sVal < 0 || cVal < 0) {
            errorMsg.classList.remove('hidden');
            totalBadge.className = "text-[10px] font-extrabold text-rose-700 bg-rose-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md";
            submitBtn.disabled = true;
        } else {
            errorMsg.classList.add('hidden');
            totalBadge.className = "text-[10px] font-extrabold theme-text-dark theme-bg-light px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border theme-border-light";
            submitBtn.disabled = false;
            finalHidden.value = `%${sVal} (Yönlendiren) - %${cVal} (Kapatan)`;
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const bInput = document.getElementById('realButceInput');
        if (bInput && bInput.value && Number(bInput.value) > 0) {
            document.getElementById('displayButce').value = Number(bInput.value).toLocaleString('tr-TR');
        }
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

======================================================================

{% block title %}Hesap Ayarları - EMLAKMATCH{% endblock %}

        </div>

    <!-- Hata Bildirimi -->
    {% if err %}
    <div class="p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-900 text-xs font-bold rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between shadow-2xs">

                {% elif err == 'support_failed' %}Destek talebi iletilemedi.

            </span>
        </div>

    </div>

    <!-- ================= 1. BİLDİRİM TERCİHLERİ ================= -->
    <div class="bg-[#F8FAF9] border border-emerald-200/90 rounded-3xl p-4 shadow-xs space-y-3">
